New shared guard `internal/netutil` (`EnforcementClassReject` + `IsAbsolutelyNonBannable`; absolute=loopback/unspecified/multicast non-overridable; default-reject=RFC1918/ULA/link-local/CGNAT/doc/reserved; public+CIDR pass; netip + prefixes; mapped normalized; v4/v6 symmetric) applied at EVERY enforcement writer: F1 `daemon_handlers_sync.go:handleSyncRequest` (class guard + `d.backend.IsExempt` before `nft.AddIPWithTimeout` — closes the file→kernel full-sync bypass), F2 `persistence/blacklistd.go:PersistBan` (refuse before file write), F3 `nftbackend/backend.go` (`Backend.Ban` class reject BEFORE the fail-open exemption + `exemptAddRejection` for `AddElement`). Design rule `parse→normalize→absolute-class reject→self/host reject→persist/apply`. Structurally non-bannable even with a nil exempt resolver. Shell/config/tests only · daemon byte-identical · 0 Go · nft schema 1.84.0 unchanged · `ModulesJSON` untouched · telemetry default-OFF · no RBL scan-behavior change · RBL NOT enabled.** `nftban rbl enable` was hard-blocked on **every** host since 2025-12-31 (`e6998e26` shellcheck sweep): `cmd_rbl.sh` runs under `IFS=$'\n\t'` and the shared `nftban_prereq_require_any_cmd` split its space-separated binary list (`host dig nslookup`) with `for bin in $binaries_str`/`read -a`, which under that IFS iterated ONCE over the joined string → DNS-tool prereq always MISSING even when the tools existed → enable refused. Fixes: (1) **IFS-safe prereq contract** — `require_any_cmd` sets a `local IFS=$' \t\n'` (auto-restored on return; RESTORES default whitespace IFS, not tampering — annotated for the Semgrep `ifs-tampering` rule); latent same-class `check_zabbix` fixed by the same helper; (2) **valid `dns_utils` package key** replaces the unmapped/invalid `bind_utils`, added to the central per-distro maps in **all 21** `etc/nftban/distros/*.conf` — `bind9-dnsutils` on apt (Debian 11–14, Ubuntu 22–26), `bind-utils` on dnf (Alma/Rocky/CentOS/CentOS-Stream 8–10, Fedora), verified empirically per distro/release. PR-A [#1071](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/1071) `9eaf3dc3` (23 files: prereq lib + 21 distro confs + test; 0 Go, no `cmd_rbl.sh`/`nftban_rbl.sh` scan logic). Test `rbl_prereq_ifs_dns_utils_v219_1_test.sh` 10/10 (strict-IFS positive→0; negative hint lists host/dig/nslookup split; IFS contract present; `dns_utils` used never `bind_utils`; exact per-distro pkg value in all 21 confs). **RELEASED + fleet-live 11/11** (tag `v1.219.1`→`12a2251d`; PACKAGE_NATIVE_PASS lab2/lab4 → canary → fleet; RBL prereq proven non-mutatively under strict IFS, `dns_utils` distro-correct, no `bind_utils`, RBL disabled/timer inactive fleet-wide); closure `CLOSED_BY_V1_219_1`. PATTERN-DATA-ONLY + test · daemon byte-identical · nft schema 1.84.0 unchanged · telemetry default-OFF · no Go · no threshold/window/ban/action-mode/enablement change.** `EXP_RFI` was the bare `=https?://` (`url-any`, thr 2) — matched ANY URL-valued query param (OAuth `redirect_uri=`, `return_url=`, `next=`, share `url=`, `utm_source=`, CDN `src=`) → banned real visitors mid-flow (RevSlider class; live on fleet, BotScan action=both). SHELL-ONLY · daemon byte-identical · nft schema 1.84.0 unchanged · telemetry default-OFF · no pattern/enforcement/enablement change.** Package-native lab2/lab4 caught a missed v1.218.11 acceptance item: `nftban botscan status` still headed `Bot Scanner Status` with no timer line (the label/timer had landed on the `botscan config` render, not the real `core/nftban_botscan.sh:nftban_botscan_status`). Fixed: heading → **`HTTP Exploit Scanner (BotScan) Status`** + a **`Timer:`** line for `nftban-botscan.timer`; Enabled/Action Mode/Patterns preserved; `botscan config` label left intact. **Carries all v1.218.11 operator-truth work** (`nftban status` HTTP Guard + HTTP Exploit Scan independent + "BotGuard disabled != BotScan disabled" note; `nftban help` exposes botscan). PR [#1063](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/1063) `12dd1579`; test `botscan_status_label_v218_12_test.sh` 10/10. **RELEASED + fleet-live 11/11 (tag `v1.218.12`→`f31fe4c3`); closure `CLOSED_BY_V1_218_12`. Superseded as fleet candidate by v1.218.13 (the current release lane above).**
**Prior release lane:** v1.218.11 (2026-07-09) — **BotScan operator-truth V1 (Track 2 shell slice). SHELL-ONLY · daemon byte-identical · nft schema 1.84.0 unchanged · telemetry default-OFF · no behavior/enforcement/pattern/enablement change.** First release applying the post-v1.218.10 operator-truth principle — **"any component that can enforce bans must be first-class in operator surfaces."** BotScan (periodic HTTP-log exploit scanner, bans via `blacklist_manual_*`) was invisible in `nftban status` (showed only `Bot Guard: DISABLED`) during the 2026-07-08 RevSlider incident. Now: `nftban status` renders **HTTP Exploit Scan** (BotScan) beside **HTTP Guard** (BotGuard) [enabled/disabled + `nftban-botscan.timer` + action mode + "BotGuard disabled != BotScan disabled" note]; `nftban help` exposes `botscan` with the Guard-vs-Exploit-Scanner distinction; `nftban botscan status` gains the HTTP Exploit Scanner label + timer line. PR [#1061](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/1061) `63e8450b`; test 14/14; shellcheck clean; regressions v128 7/0 · cli_hardening 18/0 · status_consistency 15/0. **Release lane:** v1.218.3 (2026-07-07) — **Two already-merged post-v1.218.2 SHELL-ONLY correctness/security fixes. <!-- superseded v1.216.3 lane (history in CHANGELOG; v1.216.3 published + fleet 11/11; superseded by v1.216.4 warning-quality lane):
**Release lane:** v1.216.3 (2026-07-04) — **Health-truth: validator journal query uses server-side grep — busy-host heartbeat eviction fix (OPEN_HEALTH_TRUTH_LOGINMON_JOURNAL_QUERY_LINECAP_V216_3). Rollout-unblocking follow-up to v1.216.2 (which was PUBLISHED but NOT fleet-approved: its heartbeat fixed the quiet-host false VAL-LOGINMON-001 but package validation FAILED on lab2, a busy DEB host). Root cause: the validator journal reader ran `journalctl -u nftband --since -15m -o cat -r -n 200` and matched patterns CLIENT-SIDE in Go, so `-n 200` capped total lines BEFORE matching → on a busy host (~488 nftband lines/min of [EVENT]/[BAN]) the 5-min heartbeat (2 lines/15m) was evicted from the returned window → VAL-LOGINMON-001 still fired. Fix (internal/validator/journal.go only): buildJournalArgs adds server-side `-g <regexp.QuoteMeta(p)|…>` BEFORE `-n` so the cap bounds MATCHING lines (patterns=compile-time constants, escaped, argv-only, no shell; [botguard] loaded stays literal); parseJournalResult handles `-g` no-match exit-1 as definitive absence (ErrNone/Found=false) so genuine-unbound modules still emit (hard failures stay errors). Bonus: also fixes the shared BotGuard evidence query. No LoginMon producer change. GO VALIDATOR — DAEMON/TOOL RE-BASELINE: YES (nftban-validate change; nftband relinks internal/validator; NOT byte-identical). nft schema 1.84.0 unchanged. No sysctl/nft/conntrack/loopback/profile/package change. RELEASE-PREP IN PROGRESS — PENDING TAG/PUBLISH.** (Impl PR [#1003](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/1003) → main `88eaef80`, 3 files [journal.go + 2 tests]; all 10 required CI checks SUCCESS; journal_args_test {GrepBeforeCap, RegexEscapeNoOvermatch, NonPatternNoGrep, RetainsBounds} + parseJournalResult {exit-1→absent, timeout/exec→error} + module_health TestLoginMonHighVolumeHeartbeatFound; existing journal T1-T9 green; go test ./... + vet + gofmt clean; gosec G204 on the fixed journalctl call dismissed as documented false positive [argv-only, escaped internal constants].) **NFT `SchemaVersionCurrent` UNCHANGED (1.84.0). **Release lane:** v1.216.1 (2026-07-04) — **Sysctl dead-socket guard idle-age classification + DEB conntrack fallback (OPEN_DEB_CONNTRACK_IDLE_AGE_OBSERVABILITY). Refines the v1.216.0 read-only guard: classify a local TCP DB pool by IDLE AGE vs the live established timeout — CLEAN / INFO (active or established>=keepalive) / WARN (long-idle >=50% of established timeout, established<keepalive) / UNKNOWN (unmeasurable) — instead of warning on ANY pool. Fixes the conservative false-positive on actively-polled monitoring sockets (monitor zabbix-agent2 PG plugin, idle ~45-59s << 600s). Cross-distro audit found Debian/Ubuntu kernels ship CONFIG_NF_CONNTRACK_PROCFS=n (no /proc/net/nf_conntrack) while RHEL-family has it: idle-age source order = procfs -> `conntrack -L` (optional) -> UNKNOWN. Adds DEB `Recommends: conntrack` (optional, NOT Depends; RPM unchanged, procfs full), support `idle_age_source` field, docs/SYSCTL_DEAD_SOCKET_OBSERVABILITY.md matrix. UNKNOWN is first-class/honest (never silent-CLEAN; advises apt install conntrack); watchdog elevates WARN only, never UNKNOWN. SHELL/PACKAGING/DOCS + read-only, credential-free (no DB queries), NO live sysctl write. DAEMON RE-BASELINE: NO — daemon + matcher BYTE-IDENTICAL. **Release lane:** v1.212.0 (2026-07-01) — **BotScan lost-ban-signal fix (BOTSCAN_LOST_BAN_SIGNAL): closes the BotScan lost-ban-signal P0 (enforcement loss). The producer appended to `batch_signals.jsonl` with a bare `>>` (no lock) and the daemon consumer did open→read→`os.WriteFile(signalFile, nil)` truncate-of-LIVE-file — a signal appended in that window was silently destroyed, no counter. Fix (flock-guarded rename-then-consume): the producer appends under a shared `flock` on `${signal_file}.lock` (safe degrade if flock absent; write failure visible/nonzero); the consumer no longer truncates the live file — under the SAME flock (held only around the O(1) op) it atomically renames `batch_signals.jsonl` → `.consuming`, releases the lock, then processes `.consuming` OUTSIDE the lock (bounded-tail/age/malformed/idempotent semantics unchanged), removing it on success; a prior-crash `.consuming` is recovered first each cycle. New health counters `BatchHandoffErrors` + `BatchStaleConsumingRecovered` (omitempty) → a broken handoff is WARN/DEGRADE-visible, never a false PROTECTED. **Prior release lane:** v1.198.2 (2026-06-22) — **Hotfix: firewall-transition health truth (official clear path + verdict aggregation + double-render dedup).** (Branch `release/v1.198.2`; scope `V1_198_2_SCOPE_FW_TRANSITION_HEALTH_TRUTH.md`; squash PR [#923](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/923) → main `7f763ca8`.) **Shell-only — zero `.go`, daemon byte-identical to v1.198.1, NFT schema UNCHANGED (1.84.0); no firewall/ban enforcement/detector/BotGuard change.** Surfaced when lab2 showed a sticky `floor_breach=1` CRITICAL while live protection was healthy, with no proportionate clear path and a green headline beside the CRITICAL line. **A (`BUG-FW-TRANSITION-HEALTH-COUNTER-STICKY-NO-RESET`):** new `nftban firewall transition-health [status|ack]` — `ack` zeroes the cumulative counters ONLY after a live probe verifies the mgmt floor intact (refuses otherwise — no masking), preserves anomaly history, touches only the state JSON (no nft/ban mutation, no `reset --force`, no manual edit); a clean `firewall rebuild` also clears via the same gate. **B (`BUG-HEALTH-VERDICT-IGNORES-FW-TRANSITION-CRITICAL`):** a CRITICAL transition alarm now flags `nftban health` (`PASS_WITH_WARN`/`Action: WARN` + note) + qualifies the `nftban status` Health roll-up — no more green/`PASS`/`NONE` beside `FW Transition: CRITICAL`. **C (`D-V198-HEALTH-FINDINGS-DOUBLE-RENDER`):** readiness is verdict-only; findings render once. CLI registered in commands.registry.yml + help + bash-completion. **Validation:** PR #923 CI green (53 pass / 0 fail) + post-merge main CI green (25 success / 0 fail); lab-first **lab2 (DEB) stuck-host proof PASS — real `floor_breach=1` cleared via official `ack`, bans preserved 343→343, health consistent — + lab4 (RPM) clean regression PASS**, both restored. **Prior release lane:** v1.198.1 (2026-06-22) — **Hotfix: alert-handler `/dev/log` sandbox robustness + official install_state recommit path.** (Branch `release/v1.198.1`; scope `V1_198_1_HOTFIX_SCOPE_ALERT_HANDLER_AND_RECOMMIT.md`; squash PR [#921](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/921) → main `260d0ad9`.) Surgical hotfix for two coupled defects surfaced during the v1.198.0 Track-A rollout (a host upgraded functionally but stuck `install_state=DEGRADED`). **PR-A `D-NFTBAN-ALERT-LOGGER-DEVLOG-PERMISSION`:** the OnFailure alert oneshot (`nftban-alert@.service`, `PrivateDevices=yes`+`User=nftban`) failed `logger: socket /dev/log: Permission denied`; the unguarded `logger` (last command in `log_alert()`) latched the unit failed → post-install assertion tripped → DEGRADED, and real failures went unalerted. `cli/sbin/nftban-service-alert` `log_alert()` now delivers to the journal via stderr (`StandardError=journal`+`SyslogIdentifier`, no `/dev/log` needed) + file log, makes `logger` best-effort (`2>/dev/null || true`), returns 0; sandbox UNCHANGED (not relaxed), email delivery rc contract kept (no masking). **PR-B `D-V198-STICKY-DEGRADED-NO-RECOMMIT-PATH`:** new `nftban-installer --revalidate` / `nftban update recommit` — restart-free recompute (re-runs only the live post-install assertions + rewrites install_state via the official writer; no install, no daemon restart) that moves a stale DEGRADED → COMMITTED only when every live assertion passes (refuses on non-DEGRADED/version-mismatch/no-state-file; stays DEGRADED with the live reason otherwise; never masks); plus the `nftban-alert@` template added to `staleClearableOneshotStems` so a pre-existing alert latch recovers without the live-health gate (the v1.185.1 botscan pattern; in-window failures still DEGRADE). **Go change confined to `nftban-installer` (`--revalidate` + alert-template classification); `nftband` runtime daemon otherwise unchanged. NFT schema UNCHANGED (1.84.0). No firewall/ban/detector/BotGuard/counter/metrics change.** **Validation:** PR #921 CI green (69 pass / 0 fail) + post-merge main CI green (25 success / 0 fail); lab-first **lab2 (DEB) 24/24 + lab4 (RPM) 24/24 PASS, both restored clean** (alert oneshot not-failed under real sandbox + journal/file delivery + simulated `/dev/log` EACCES exits 0; recommit DEGRADED→COMMITTED + 4 no-mask negative controls; alert not re-failed on restart). Tests `systemd_payload_v1981_test.go` + `revalidate_test.go` + `alert_logger_devlog_sandbox_v1981_test.sh`. **Prior release lane:** v1.192.2 (2026-06-17) — **BotScan authenticated WordPress admin/editor false-positive hotfix.** (Branch `fix/v1.192.2-botscan-wpadmin-fp`, squash PR [#885](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/885) → main `2fff7af4`; scope `BOTSCAN_WP_AUTHENTICATED_ADMIN_FALSE_POSITIVE_SCOPE.md`.) A legitimately logged-in WordPress admin using Gutenberg/Elementor was classified `request_class=scanner` (the block editor fetches `/wp-json/wp/v2/users` → `EXP_WPREST`; editor calls trip `WS_WPADMIN`) → score 80 → ban → escalation to the permanent blacklist (confirmed srv4 / `nafpliotisbros.gr`). v1.192.2 adds a **per-IP authenticated-context gate**: a successful login (`POST /wp-login.php → 302`; uses the already-parsed HTTP status — a failed/probing login returns 200) suppresses **only** the configured WP admin/REST scanner pattern contribution (`BOTSCAN_WPADMIN_CONTEXT_PATTERNS`, default `EXP_WPREST WS_WPADMIN`) for that IP before the threshold check. **Authenticated WP-admin/editor false-positives are reduced; unauthenticated `/wp-json` REST enumeration, exploit/webshell/CVE, no-auth `WS_WPADMIN`, and empty/rotating-UA scanners are ALL still detected and banned** (no global `EXP_WPREST`/`WS_WPADMIN` weakening; v1.189 verified-crawler semantics unchanged; BotGuard/LoginMon untouched). **Shell-only product change — Go source byte-identical to v1.192.1** (packaged `nftban-core`/`nftband` hashes move only via the embedded git-commit stamp, not a code change). **NFT schema UNCHANGED (1.84.0).** LOG_SOURCE_OWNERSHIP_DECLARATION = ACCEPTED (same access-log event class BotScan already owns; no new source/identity; zero-input unchanged). Knobs `BOTSCAN_WPADMIN_CONTEXT_GATE`/`_CONTEXT_PATTERNS`/`_LOGIN_PATH` (default-on). **Validation:** hermetic `botscan_wpadmin_context_gate_v1922_test.sh` 8/8 + lab2 DEB + lab4 RPM full `process_logs` replay + **DEB/RPM package-native validation PASS** (`V192_2_PACKAGE_NATIVE_PASS_READY_FOR_PR`); post-merge main CI green. **Prior release lane:** v1.192.1 (2026-06-17) — **Firewall service-port transition atomicity (residual fix) + PR-B firewall transition health.** (Branch `fix/v1.192.1-service-port-set-atomicity`; scope `V1_192_1_FUNCTIONAL_HOTFIX_SCOPE.md`.) **Closes the residual service-port transition gap that left v1.192.0 as PARTIAL MITIGATION.** v1.192.0 closed the rebuild floor-collapse (F-RB) + blacklist refresh (F-FEED/F-GEO), but the durable nftables config still rendered **skeletal** service-port sets (`tcp_ports_in/out`, `udp_ports_in/out`) and relied on a post-load daemon sync — so a configured port (e.g. `993`) could be momentarily absent from the live sets immediately after a rebuild/reload (~1.85s drop window; lab-proven cross-family). v1.192.1 renders the **complete** effective service ports **declaratively inside the nft set blocks** (`elements = {…}`, ip+ip6) from the same authority the daemon uses (`ports.LoadAllPorts`), so the atomic `nft -f` installs complete `tcp_ports_in/out` + `udp_ports_in/out` **before** any daemon sync; rebuild/reload **fail-closed** if the effective-port render fails (never applies a skeletal ruleset). Adds CI guard `V-NFT-SERVICE-PORTS-RENDERED-COMPLETE` + **PR-B harm-keyed firewall transition health** — `service_port_breach_count` / `floor_breach_count` / `table_absent_while_committed_count` / `blacklist_empty_during_refresh_count` / `non_atomic_rebuild_count` in a state JSON (`/var/lib/nftban/state/firewall_transition_health.json`, **NOT the NFT schema**), surfaced as a `nftban health` finding (anomalous-only) + a `nftban status` "FW Transition" line + `--json`; rebuild cadence never alarms; healthy transitions keep all counters zero. **NFT schema UNCHANGED (1.84.0). `nftban-core` rebuilt; daemon hash MOVED (the branch adds `internal/ports/effective.go`, linked into `nftband`) — nftband runtime behavior unchanged (sync still uses the unchanged `ports.LoadAllPorts`; no daemon path calls the new render funcs).** **Validation:** functional rebuild+reload lab proof (lab2 DEB + lab4 RPM, v4/v6 probes, 0 excess drops, `tcp_ports_in` kept the configured port through the transition, durable complete before daemon sync) + PR-B health lab proof (healthy → zero counters; controlled missing-port injection → reports the exact set/family/port; `health`/`status` surfaces validated) + **DEB/RPM package-native validation PASS** (real packages install the new `nftban-core` + shell helpers + health files + tests on lab2/lab4 and reproduce the runtime behavior; binary consistency RPM≡DEB; verdict `V192_1_PACKAGE_NATIVE_PASS_READY_FOR_RELEASE_PREP`). Release-prep touches `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header version only). **No schema bump · no counter population in the NFT schema.** Next: PR merge after green main (separate gate), then tag/publish.

**Prior release lane:** v1.192.0 (2026-06-17) — **Firewall transition atomicity (F-RB + F-FEED + F-GEO) — PARTIAL MITIGATION (residual service-port transition gap closed by v1.192.1).** (PR [#881](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/881), squash-merged `c926f22e`; scope `V1_192_FIREWALL_CONTINUITY_SCOPE.md` + audit `V1_192_AUDIT_NFTBAN_TRANSITION_CONTINUITY_BY_MODULE_AND_TIMER.md`.) **Daemon-Go + shell fix — daemon hash MOVED (Go set-writer change in `internal/setsync`, linked into `nftband`); NFT schema UNCHANGED (1.84.0); `MergeStats`/IPC contracts unchanged; no counter population.** Closes the nftables transition-atomicity class — *flush + repopulate in separate transactions* replaced with one atomic `nft -f`. **F-RB** (fail-CLOSED): removed the standalone `nft flush table ip/ip6 nftban` before the separate `nft -f` in `_firewall_rebuild_core` (the rendered config already self-resets atomically: `table{}`→`delete table`→recreate), eliminating the rebuild drop window (lab repro: input chain collapsed 19→1; post-fix never breaches the management floor). **F-FEED/F-GEO** (fail-OPEN): the feed/geoban refresh flushed the shared `blacklist_*` set then repopulated separately (banned IPs admitted during the window) — new `replaceSetElementsViaFile`/`renderSetReplaceScript` emit `flush set` as the first line of the *same* `nft -f` script (atomic; rolls back fail-CLOSED on error); the two interval split/refresh paths route through it; `nftFlushSet` removed. Static guards `check-nft-atomicity.sh` (V-NFT-REBUILD-ATOMICITY + V-NFT-SET-REFRESH-ATOMICITY, function-scoped, FAIL-on-pre-fix-verified) + hermetic Go ordering test + lab-first runtime tests. **Validation:** lab2 DEB + lab4 RPM source+daemon deploy restored clean — F-RB management-floor breach 0/600 + 0/500 during rebuild; F-FEED/F-GEO atomic replace never empty; PR #881 CI green 57/1/0. Release-prep touches `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header version only). **No schema bump · no counter population · recovery/reset/rollback/restore paths exempt by design.** Next: tag/publish v1.192.0 (separate gate); **PR-B harm-keyed health observability → v1.192.1**; live populated feed/geoban refresh at rollout.

**Prior release lane:** v1.191.0 (2026-06-16) — **8B BotGuard request-class-aware tuning + bounded daemon-Go decision cache.** (PR [#879](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/879), squash-merged `d526da6f`.) **Daemon-Go change — daemon hash MOVED off the prior `351d35df` byte-identical baseline (8B is daemon-Go); NFT schema UNCHANGED (1.84.0); NO counter population (counters-populate lane → v1.192.0+); BotGuard default remains DISABLED pending a separate canary + re-enable gate.** Fixes the L4/request-blind browser / e-shop / gallery / WooCommerce-admin false-positive class: keep+raise the browser-safe L4 meter (suspect 100/s burst 200; grey 25/50; pending 50/100), structured `family`/`request_class` batch signals, BotScan request_class classifier, temporary daemon-Go decision cache (6 temporary states, monotonic TTL, bounded caps + eviction, IPv6 /64 candidate accounting), browser-like batch-signal suppression with dynamic_abuse/login_api/scanner enforcement preserved, cadence-gap gate (`ACCEPT_WITH_VISIBLE_LAG`), read-only `explain_ip`/`search`/`emulate`/`debug botguard`/`support`/`health` observability, bounded restart warm-up, operator config knobs (`HTTP_BOT_CACHE_*`/`HTTP_BOT_WARMUP_*`/`HTTP_BOT_CADENCE_STALE_AFTER_SECONDS`), and the BotGuard disable-orphan-chain fix. **Validation:** inc1–8A hermetic PASS · lab2 DEB + lab4 RPM behavioral source-deploy `PASS_RESTORE_CLEAN` · CI package-native build/install `PASS_WITH_WARNINGS_READY_FOR_PR` · PR #879 CI green · post-merge main CI green. **Prior release lane:** v1.189.0 (2026-06-15) — **BotScan FCrDNS: verified-crawler whitelist (forward-confirmed reverse DNS, analyze-time).** (PR [#866](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/866); scope `BOTSCAN_VERIFIED_CRAWLER_WHITELIST_SCOPE.md`.) **Security/evasion fix. Shell-only; daemon byte-identical `c63d8822`; schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO counters (deferred to SCHEMA-UNFREEZE).** Closes the spoofable UA-substring crawler whitelist (any client sending `User-Agent: Googlebot` was blanket-whitelisted → evaded BotScan). **Fix:** at **analyze-time, per unique candidate IP — never per log line** (preserves the v1.187.1 fork-free hot path), a claimed search-crawler is forward-confirmed-rDNS verified (PTR matches a provider-documented family suffix → forward-resolve returns the original IP). **Fail-closed:** no PTR / suffix mismatch / forward mismatch / timeout / NXDOMAIN ⇒ NOT verified. A **verified** crawler is exempt from the **404-flood ban ONLY** (real crawlers legitimately hit 404s); a spoofer is banned with a `fake_bot_ua` reason. **Not a blanket IP whitelist** — exploit/webshell/scanner URL-pattern bans are NEVER exempted by crawler-verify (operator constraint). Cache by `src_ip+family` (24h/6h/30m), atomic in-flight guard, hard per-lookup timeouts (2s), self-contained resolver (host→dig→nslookup), `BOTSCAN_VERIFY_CRAWLERS` toggle (default on); unverifiable families (facebot) keep the legacy whitelist. **Validation:** hermetic `botscan_fcrdns_v189_test` (stubbed resolver) PASS — verify matrix, fail-closed, cache/family-separation, toggle, no-per-line-DNS, exempt-verified/ban-spoofer, structural guard; botscan regressions pass; shellcheck clean; daemon `c63d8822`; **lab-first real-DNS PASS** (lab2: real Googlebot `66.249.66.1` verified OK + cached; `8.8.8.8` claiming Googlebot fail-closed — caught+fixed a resolver-dependency bug). **Prior release lane:** v1.187.2 (2026-06-15) — **BotScan-train B1: CLI/text-UX (banner tagline + update-lock friendliness).** (PR [#860](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/860).) **Presentation only — shell/data, daemon byte-identical `c63d8822`; no schema/packaging/detection-content change.** Two operator-requested UX-friendliness items: **UX-BANNER-TEXT** — the unified banner tagline is now Firewall-first, `Open-source Linux IPS & nftables FW` → `Open-source Linux Firewall & IPS for nftables` (drops the informal "FW", avoids the "inline IPS" misread; nftables stays the named authority), plus `NFTBAN_VERSION_NAME` (`nftban version` JSON) `Linux IPS & nftables Firewall` → `Linux Firewall & IPS for nftables`. **UX-UPDATE-LOCK** — the lock-contention path (`cmd_update.sh`) replaces the terse "Another update is in progress / clear stale lock" with a friendly message that best-effort names the in-progress updater (PID + start time via fuser/lsof, excluding our own fd-9 holder, only when alive), points to `tail -f /var/log/nftban/update.log`, and clarifies `nftban update force` clears only a STALE lock (after a crash/reboot); the flock mechanism is unchanged. **Deferred to B1b (need a design decision):** BUG-BANNER-INCONSISTENT (two renderers — `nftban_banner_unified` box vs `nftban_render_banner_simple` 2-line; root cause pinned: the main dispatcher sets `NFTBAN_MOTTO='ban · unban · protect'` consumed only by the simple renderer) and UX-VERBOSE-FINDINGS; VAL-LOGINMON-002-UX is validator-Go (separate). **Validation:** new hermetic `b1_text_ux_v1872_test.sh` (behavioral banner render asserts the Firewall-first tagline + `NFTBAN_VERSION_NAME` + update-lock content guards) PASS; shellcheck `-S warning` clean; daemon byte-identical `c63d8822`; schema 1.83.0 frozen; FHS `--check` rc=0. **Prior release lane:** v1.187.1 (2026-06-14) — **Hotfix: BotScan cycle-timeout regression on high-log-count hosts (main-loop per-line fork removal; A4 404-tail bound retained).** (PR [#858](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/858); diagnosis `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_187_1_TIMEOUT_ROOT_CAUSE_DIAGNOSIS.md`; srv2 proof `V1_187_1_SRV2_VALIDATION_RECORD.md`.) **v1.187.1 fixes the BotScan processor cycle-timeout regression on high-log-count hosts by removing the main-scan per-line subshell-fork overhead, preserving the A4 404-tail bound, and lowering the per-file cap. It does NOT change Lane B, aibots, BotGuard counters, schema, daemon Go, or UX.** The v1.187.0 fleet rollout was NO-GO on srv2 (AlmaLinux 9.8, DirectAdmin, 126 access logs/921 MB): the BotScan cycle SIGTERM'd at the 300s `TimeoutStartSec`. Timing markers pinned the cost to the **main scan loop**, not A4/analyze — a single 4.2 MB DA log took **123s / 5497 lines ≈ 22 ms/line**, dominated by **three per-line command-substitution forks**: `parsed=$(parse_line)`, `matched=$(match_url)` (in `process_entry`), and `now=$(nftban_timestamp_unix||date)`. The soft budget (`BOTSCAN_SCAN_BUDGET_SECS=180`) is checked **between files only**, so one slow file straddling the boundary overran toward 300s; v1.187 Lane A's forward cursor (≤1 MiB ≈ 5k lines/file vs the old `tail -1000`) exposed the latent fork cost. A4 `count_404_tail` measured **0s** (the bound fires) and `analyze` ~1s. **Fix (shell/data only):** `nftban_botscan_parse_line` → no-fork `nftban_botscan_parse_line_g` (returns fields via `_BS_IP/_BS_URL/_BS_METHOD/_BS_STATUS/_BS_UA` globals); the echo-API `parse_line` is kept as a thin wrapper delegating to `_g` so `cmd_botscan` + existing tests cannot drift. `nftban_botscan_match_url` → no-fork `nftban_botscan_match_url_g` (`_BS_MATCHED`), echo wrapper kept. `process_entry` timestamp → fork-free `printf -v now '%(%s)T' -1` (bash 4.2+; old fork fallback). Cheap backstop: `BOTSCAN_SCAN_MAX_BYTES_PER_FILE` default 1 MiB → 256 KiB (~1.3k lines) so no single file can approach the hard timeout; the rotation cursor still covers every file across cycles. **The A4 404-tail budget bound (per-file soft-deadline + total-bytes backstop + `404-rotate` cursor) is retained.** Preserves parse semantics, URL/UA match semantics (all match types), 404-flood detection, the v1.186.1 IFS pattern-ban fix, signal format, and thresholds/windows/durations. **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.186 `c63d8822`** (shell/data only; canonical `CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -trimpath -buildvcs=false ./cmd/nftband` reproduces it); schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS change (header `meta:version` only). **Validation:** new hermetic `botscan_nofork_parity_v1871_test.sh` (parse_line_g == echo across IPv4/IPv6/bracketed/no-UA/malformed; match_url_g == echo across every match type + no-match) + `botscan_404_tail_bound_v1871_test.sh` + v187 throughput + v185 deadline/rotation + v1.186.1 IFS + http_log_discovery_v177 (echo API) regressions PASS; shellcheck `-S warning` clean. **srv2 production proof 3/3** (reversible single-file swap): cycles 209s/185s/183s all `Result=success` status 0, none near 300s (256 KiB caps per-file ~30s → worst-case overrun one file); throughput ~2× (13.4k → 25.6k entries/cycle); daemon active NRestarts=0, 0 nftban failed units, nft authority intact, 0 TEST-NET leak, 0 bans (no false-positive storm). PR #858 CI green. Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). Next: tag/publish v1.187.1, then fleet rollout (re-arm srv2 `nftban-botscan.timer`). **NEXT lane = v1.187 Lane B BOTSCAN-BADBOT-EASY-UX** (HOLD until explicit GO + ownership declaration + packaging guardrail); adaptive promotion HOLD; FCrDNS v1.188; BotGuard counters = separate schema-unfreeze lane. (Full backlog-drain throughput remains a separate lane — the residual per-line cost is now the 137-pattern match loop, not forks.)

**Prior release lane:** v1.187.0 (2026-06-14) — **BotScan throughput (Lane A): BOTSCAN-SCAN-THROUGHPUT — forward cursor + candidate prefilter + 404 fixed-tail.** (PR [#855](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/855); plan `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_187_IMPL_PLAN_CODE_CHALLENGE.md` + `V1_187_DECISION_ADDENDUM.md`; validation `V1_187_LANE_A_VALIDATION_RECORD.md`.) Closes the flood-host throughput class v1.185's per-file cap left as a KNOWN LIMITATION (tail-biased → dropped the majority of bytes). **A1 forward-mode per-file cursor** (`nftban_http_read_incremental` opt-in `NFTBAN_HTTP_LOG_READ_FORWARD`): emits the oldest unread window and advances the offset by exactly what it emits → drains a backlog FORWARD across cycles with **no skipped bytes**, on a dedicated `proc-offsets` dir (distinct from the collector, which stays byte-identical). Per-file cap default 64 KiB→1 MiB. **A2 candidate prefilter**: one C-speed `grep -E -f` pass (sound ERE superset of the matcher, DFA → no ReDoS) drops the measured 49–90% droppable fraction before the ~55-eps bash loop (srv2 high-water host: **77% dropped**, 696,883→159,124). **A3 regex hygiene** (literal/regex split; no combined alternation). **A4 404-window Option 1**: independent fixed-tail re-read, never reads/rewinds the cursor offset, preserves 404-burst detection. Per-cycle wall-time stays bounded by the processor's soft deadline (`BOTSCAN_SCAN_BUDGET_SECS=180` < `TimeoutStartSec=300`) → never SIGTERM'd. **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.186 `c63d8822`** (shell/data only); schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS change (header `meta:version` only). **Validation:** hermetic `botscan_throughput_v187_test.sh` PASS 3/3 (forward no-skip, prefilter soundness/no ban regression, 404 fixed-tail independence) + v185 regression + v1.186.1 IFS test + shellcheck clean; real-host srv2 prefilter 77% drop; bounded-by-design (processor 180/300s); cross-distro/uutils via the v1.186.1 rollout. PR #855 CI green. Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). Next: tag/publish v1.187.0. **NEXT lane = v1.187 Lane B BOTSCAN-BADBOT-EASY-UX** (`bots`/`blockbot`/`allowbot`, `aibots.patterns` with primary-doc-verified tokens, `override.local` config(noreplace)); adaptive promotion HOLD; FCrDNS v1.188; BotGuard counters = separate schema-unfreeze lane.

**Prior release lane:** v1.186.1 (2026-06-14) — **Hotfix: BotScan URL/UA pattern-ban correctness (IFS word-split).** (PR [#853](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/853); finding `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_187_FINDING_BOTSCAN_PATTERN_BAN_IFS.md`; lab record `V1_186_1_LAB_VALIDATION_RECORD.md`.) **v1.186.1 fixes BotScan URL/UA pattern ban correctness under the production runtime IFS and restores catalog/custom pattern ban behavior; it does NOT fix BotScan throughput (`BOTSCAN-SCAN-THROUGHPUT` remains open for v1.187) or bot-policy UX (`BOTSCAN-BOT-POLICY-UX` remains open for v1.187).** The lib sets a global `IFS=$'\n\t'` (no space) at source time and the processor (`cli/sbin/nftban-botscan-processor`) inherits it; `nftban_botscan_analyze` iterated the per-IP matched-pattern list with an unquoted `for pattern_name in $patterns` over a space-joined value (`" NAME"`), so the leading space was kept → the pattern key lookup missed → the per-pattern **threshold/window/ban-duration were never applied** (analyze fell back to defaults 5/60/1800). Effect (measured, no overclaim): `threshold=1/2` scanner/exploit/webshell patterns (sqlmap, nikto, Log4Shell/CVE_*, webshells WS_*) failed to ban on the first/second hit; high-volume crawler matches still emitted a weaker default **grey** signal; the **404-flood path was unaffected** (quoted IP-key iteration). Fix: IFS-independent split (`IFS=$' \t\n' read -ra`), the idiom already used for the http-log override split (`nftban_http_logs.sh`). New hermetic `botscan_pattern_ban_ifs_v1861_test.sh` (useragent + url-404 ban on first hit under the lib's real runtime IFS; fails pre-fix, passes post-fix); v185 regression + shellcheck clean. **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.186 `c63d8822`** (shell-only); schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS change (header `meta:version` only). **Validation:** A/B lab-first across all 9 fleet hosts (Ubuntu 22.04/24.04/26.04, CentOS Stream 10, AlmaLinux 9.8/10.1; GNU + uutils coreutils) — installed=NO_BAN → fixed=BAN(pattern), 0 nftban failed units, nft authority intact, signal-only/zero-enforcement/reserved IP. Follow-up (separate, NOT this hotfix): `TAIL_OBSOLETE_SYNTAX_UUTILS` (Ubuntu 26.04 uutils rejects `tail -1000` in the pinned/interactive path; production incremental reader unaffected). Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). Next: tag/publish v1.186.1.

**Prior release lane:** v1.186.0 (2026-06-14) — **Roundcube webmail auth source (LoginMon) — DirectAdmin central `/var/log/roundcube` layout only.** (PR [#851](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/851), squash `23600beb`; decision `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_186_ROUNDCUBE_DA_ONLY_DECISION.md`; ownership `ROUNDCUBE_WEBMAIL_AUTH_SOURCE_OWNERSHIP_DECLARATION.md`; gate-0 `ROUNDCUBE_GATE0_CAPTURE_RECORD.md`; lab-first `V1_186_ROUNDCUBE_LABFIRST_VALIDATION_RECORD.md`.) Adds a new LoginMon source owning ONLY the Roundcube webmail interactive **auth_failure** event class from the central `/var/log/roundcube/userlogins.log` (`internal/loginmon/detector/roundcube.go`, reason `ReasonRoundcubeAuthFail=5003`; discovery `roundcubediscovery.go`; `[LOGINMON] roundcube` input-state in `module.go`). **Matches `Failed login for … from <IP> …` only**; `Successful login` never produces a verdict (prefilter + explicit guard + test). **MANDATORY public-IP-only guard** (`isPublicLoginIP`) rejects loopback/RFC1918/link-local/ULA/multicast/unspecified/malformed BEFORE any score/ban, so the fleet-wide binary is a safe no-op on proxied/non-default hosts that log 127.0.0.1 and the dovecot `rip=localhost` webmail mirror is auto-neutralized. Root `nftband.service` (CAP_DAC_OVERRIDE) reads the `drwx--x---` webapps log directly (Option A). **Coverage claim (narrow):** DirectAdmin hosts using the central `/var/log/roundcube/userlogins.log` layout. **NOT claimed:** all DirectAdmin Roundcube installs · cPanel · Plesk · app-local `log_dir` installs (e.g. RC with unset `log_dir` → `<install>/logs/`) · all webmail brute-force coverage. (srv4 = DA + RC 1.6.15 unset `log_dir` proves the app-local caveat — a safe no-op, queued for the v1.186.x config-driven `log_dir` refinement.) **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` hash MOVES (expected; daemon-Go) `dc4e1a33`→`c63d8822`** (first daemon-Go since v1.185; canonical `CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -trimpath -buildvcs=false ./cmd/nftband` reproduces it). **Schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change** (release-prep header `meta:version` only). **Validation:** hermetic detector + discovery suites green; independent source-ownership audit PASS (`READY_FOR_MERGE=YES`); **lab-first reversible daemon-swap across 9 hosts, 0 live bans** — dns1 (Ubuntu 24.04, DA 1.7.1) WARN_NO_LOGS→OK→2 public detections (IPv4+IPv6) with localhost+RFC1918 rejected and `Successful login` ignored; dns2/srv1/srv2/srv3/srv4 WARN_NO_LOGS; lab2 (Plesk) + lab4 (cPanel) + monitor `NO_LOGS no_roundcube` clean no-op; cross-distro Ubuntu 22/24/26 + CentOS Stream 9/10 + AlmaLinux 9.8/10.1. **Prior release lane:** v1.185.1 (2026-06-14) — **Hotfix: botscan state-dir + stale-oneshot recovery + reset-failed guidance.** (PR [#849](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/849); scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_185_1_HOTFIX_SCOPE.md`; user guide `USER_GUIDE_DEGRADED_BOTSCAN_AFTER_UPGRADE.md`.) Forward hotfix for the false DEGRADED on upgrade to v1.185 (proven on monitor: the v1.185 botscan processor completes in ~6s rc=0, but a STALE v1.184 300s-timeout failed-state latched *before* the upgrade flipped install validation to DEGRADED, and `--repair` alone could not clear it). **H1 (BUG-1):** `/var/lib/nftban/botscan` (nftban:nftban 0750) added to the FHS spec + regenerated tmpfiles/dirs/fhs_spec, so the `User=nftban` processor can persist its `scan-rotate` rotation cursor (the in-script `mkdir` failed silently under the root:nftban 0750 parent → rotation never advanced; on monitor the `scan-rotate.tmp: No such file` error is now gone with the dir present). **H2:** the v1.174 stale failed-unit classification is relaxed for the stale-clearable oneshot processor (`nftban-botscan.service`) — a failure strictly BEFORE the install window recovers WITHOUT the (circular) live-health gate that the oneshot's own latch could trip; real in-window failures still DEGRADE (5 unit tests). **H3:** the DEGRADED remediation text (`cmd_update.sh` + `nftban-installer`) now surfaces `systemctl reset-failed nftban-botscan.service` + a run ahead of `--repair`, noting `--repair` alone won't clear a stale oneshot latch. **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.185 `dc4e1a33`** (installer-Go + shell + FHS-generated only); schema 1.83.0 frozen; FHS body adds one dir (`--check` rc=0). **Validation:** lab2 (DEB) + lab4 (RPM/EL9) reversible swap — H1 (dir 0750 nftban:nftban via tmpfiles) + H1-functional (scan-rotate persists) + H2 (induced stale botscan → re-validate COMMITTED, no manual reset, not masked) + H3 (text) + reboot persistence, all PASS; monitor (v1.185.0) direct — dir-present run writes scan-rotate, the prior `No such file` error eliminated. srv2 rotation-advance inconclusive (host on v1.184, times out — a v1.187 throughput boundary, not a v1.185.1 failure). **Prior release lane:** v1.185.0 (2026-06-14) — **Installer restart-debt (Lane A) + BotScan processor timeout-at-scale (Lane B).** (PR [#847](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/847); scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_185_INSTALLER_CORRECTNESS_PARITY_SCOPE.md`; records `V1_185_LANE_A_INSTALLER_RESTART_VALIDATION_RECORD.md` + `V1_185_LANE_B_BOTSCAN_FLEET_VALIDATION_RECORD.md`.) **Lane A — INSTALL-UPGRADE-NO-DAEMON-RESTART:** on an upgrade over a live daemon, the installer's `systemctl start` is a no-op (systemd won't cycle an active unit), so the OLD binary kept running with the new files on disk (proven on dns2 during the v1.184 fleet rollout). Fix: `StartDaemon` (`internal/installer/services/daemon.go`) captures `wasActive := ServiceActive("nftband.service")` up front and, only on the upgrade-over-live-daemon path, issues a new `ServiceTryRestart` (`systemctl try-restart` — cycles iff active, no-op if deliberately stopped) to load the upgraded binary; fresh installs (inactive) skip it. One shared `phaseConfigure` path covers **DEB + RPM + source** (package-family parity). Mock executor + two unit tests (upgrade→try-restart, fresh→none). **Lane B — CORE-BOTSCAN-PROCESSOR-TIMEOUT-AT-SCALE:** `nftban-botscan-processor` (Type=oneshot, `TimeoutStartSec=300`) did a whole-spool single pass every cycle; on high-volume hosts it couldn't finish in 300s → SIGTERM (status=15) → never banned + `OnFailure` every cycle. Fix (shell, `nftban_botscan.sh` `nftban_botscan_process_logs` + `cli/sbin/nftban-botscan-processor`): per-file byte cap (`BOTSCAN_SCAN_MAX_BYTES_PER_FILE`, default 64 KiB, tail-biased via the shared incremental reader) + a soft deadline checked **between files** (`BOTSCAN_SCAN_BUDGET_SECS`, default 180) that checkpoints a rotation cursor and exits rc=0 to resume next cycle → never SIGTERM'd; `analyze()` always runs. CI test `botscan_deadline_rotation_v185_test.sh`. **Scope discipline (locked, no overclaim):** Lane B fixes the processor **liveness/completion** class (never-completes / SIGTERM / alert-per-cycle) — it does **NOT** claim full-spool per-cycle coverage, fleet-health, or high-throughput ban yield; it scans the newest ~64 KiB/file/cycle. **`BOTSCAN-SCAN-THROUGHPUT` remains OPEN** as a separate follow-up lane (v1.187; bash per-line matcher ~46-65 entries/sec vs ~99 MB spool needs a C-speed prefilter). **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` is BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.184 `dc4e1a33`** — Lane A is in `cmd/nftban-installer` (not the daemon import graph) and Lane B is shell-only. **Schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change** (FHS body unchanged; release-prep header `meta:version` only). **Validation:** Lane A lab-first — lab2 (DEB) + lab4 (RPM) + lab2 (source) all CYCLE the daemon on upgrade via try-restart, lab2 reboot clean, SSH + firewall preserved, no package-family divergence. Lane B real-host reversible-shell-swap across all 9 hosts — srv2/srv4/dns2/srv3 cured (SIGTERM/never-completes → rc=0/success); lab2/lab4/monitor/srv1/dns1 no regression. PR #847 CI green. Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). **Prior release lane:** v1.184.0 (2026-06-13) — **Suricata EVE rotation fix + Train-1 detection-tail closure (NO new ban surface; NO new source).** (PR [#845](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/845), sq `d0daee92`; classification `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/TRAIN1_INPUT_AUTHORITY_DETECTION_TAIL_CLASSIFICATION.md`.) **What:** closes **SURICATA-EVE-ROTATION-MISS** — `internal/suricata/reader.go` `reopenIfRotated()` is called at the top of `drainEvents` (both inotify + polling paths) and handles **copytruncate** (`size < offset` → `Seek(0)` + reader reset) and **rename/create** (`os.SameFile` false → reopen + read from start); best-effort, stat/open errors keep the current fd and retry next tick. Tests: copytruncate, rename, no-rotation guard. **Train-1 detection tail (register-only):** **DET-WEBAUTH already closed by v1.179** (`webauth.go` parses generic apache/nginx 401; LiteSpeed shares the combined access-log format) — no code here. **Roundcube is DEFERRED** as a future new-source lane `ROUNDCUBE_WEBMAIL_AUTH_SOURCE` (needs logging prereqs + parser + watcher + dedup + `LOG_SOURCE_OWNERSHIP_DECLARATION`); direct IMAP/POP3 brute stays covered by dovecot — **Roundcube coverage is NOT claimed.** **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` is BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.183 `dc4e1a33`** (the suricata EVE reader is not in the daemon import graph — suricata module dormant; the fix lands in the suricata processor path, correctness-when-enabled). **Schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change.** **Validation:** suricata rotation units + `go build ./...` green; PR #845 CI green (0 fail; 0 GHAS-check fail). Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). **Prior release lane:** v1.183.0 (2026-06-13) — **LoginMon input-readability finding (HEALTH-NO-INPUT-AXIS increment 2; NO new ban surface).** (PR [#843](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/843); scope/design `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_182_GLOBAL_HEALTH_INPUT_AXIS_SCOPE.md`.) **What:** completes the `HEALTH-NO-INPUT-AXIS` program in a **schema-safe** way. The validator already queries the journal (`queryJournal`), so `evaluateLoginMon` (`internal/validator/module_health.go`) reads the daemon's own `[LOGINMON] <src>: state=...` startup lines (v1.179 webauth + v1.180 ftpauth), derives an internal input-readability state, and emits the `VAL-LOGINMON-002` warning finding (`CodeLoginMonNoInput`) when an enabled module's source reports no input — so `nftban health` surfaces an enabled-but-starved LoginMon source instead of reading healthy. **The frozen M81-6 health-output schema was intentionally NOT extended** — `ModuleHealth.Input` stays an INTERNAL field (`json:"-"`); a first-class JSON Input axis is **deferred to the SCHEMA-UNFREEZE major.** **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` source moves (validator compiled in) `6fdfd975`→`dc4e1a33`; schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change.** Tag obj `5c6e336e`; main merge `26b76f0e`. (Full detail in `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_183_0_RELEASE_CLOSURE.md`.)

**Prior release lane:** v1.182.0 (2026-06-13) — **LoginMon INPUT-STATE OBSERVABILITY (HEALTH-NO-INPUT-AXIS, increment 1; NO new ban surface).** (PR [#841](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/841), sq `b25ce51e`; scope/design `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_182_GLOBAL_HEALTH_INPUT_AXIS_SCOPE.md`.) **What:** the `DETECTION_INPUT_AUTHORITY` audit flagged that an enabled module reading nothing reads healthy. The validator `internal/validator/module_health.go` derives the 4-axis health from the kernel ruleset + service state and never queries daemon IPC, so it cannot see watcher starvation; the daemon (root `nftband.service`) is the authority on its own watchers. This increment captures LoginMon per-source input-state (`Module.inputSources`, mu-guarded; `recordInputState`/`inputStateSnapshot`) at watcher startup for **webauth** (v1.179) + **ftpauth** (v1.180) with the shared `OK`/`WARN_NO_LOGS`/`NO_LOGS` vocabulary, surfaced via `LoginMonStatusExtra.InputSources` (json `input_sources`, `omitempty`) + `ToExtraInfo` over the EXISTING daemon module-status IPC — **byte-equivalent wire format for existing readers when nothing is captured** — so an enabled-but-starved source is observable. **Increment 2 (next lane, NOT here):** fold this daemon-known input-state into the validator's `ModuleHealth` as a first-class **Input axis** surfaced by `nftban health` (needs a CLI→daemon health bridge) + extend to BotGuard/other modules. **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` source moves loginmon-only (`b9a462a4`→`6fdfd975`; additive); schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change.** **Validation:** unit tests (snapshot round-trip + defensive copy, Status surfacing, omitted-when-empty); `go test -race ./...` + `go build ./...` green; PR #841 CI green (53 pass/1 skip/0 fail; 0 GHAS). **Prior release lane:** v1.181.0 (2026-06-13) — **LoginMon AUTH-SOURCE dead-key cleanup + duplicate-source guard (hygiene/guardrail; NO new ban surface).** (PR [#839](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/839), sq `88280fb5`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_181_AUTH_SOURCE_DEAD_KEY_AND_DUPLICATE_GUARD_SCOPE.md`.) **What:** removes two proven-dead distro-config keys with zero readers (`pureftpd_log` + `exim_reject_log`) from all 21 `etc/nftban/distros/*.conf` (+ the orphaned FTP section comment), the validator `REQUIRED_FIELDS` (`cli/lib/nftban/tests/validate_distro_configs.sh`), and `internal/loginmon/distroconf/distroconf_test.go` — only `directadmin_login_log` / `exim_log` / `dovecot_log` are consumed via `DistroKey`; pure-ftpd has been journal facility-11 since v1.180 and exim uses `mainlog`. Adds a **duplicate-source guard** (`internal/loginmon/source_ownership_guard_test.go`) that fails CI if a future change wires a known duplicate/non-auth source into the LoginMon ban path without a fresh `LOG_SOURCE_OWNERSHIP_DECLARATION` + non-duplicate proof: **cphulkd `login_log` NOT in `panelLogPaths`** (duplicates cPanel access_log-401, PanelDetector-owned), **exim `rejectlog` NOT in `mailLogPaths`** (auth subset duplicates `mainlog`; unique lines are spam/RBL/HELO = `web_abuse`, not auth), **FTP file globs exclude pure-ftpd** (journal-routed) + any `rejectlog`, and **journal facilities pinned to `{auth 4, authpriv 10, ftp 11}`** (extracted to `journalAuthFacilities` so the set is testable). **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` source moves loginmon-only (`cce992a1`→`b9a462a4`; journal-watcher arg refactor, no behavior change); schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change.** **Validation:** `go test -race ./...` + `go build ./...` green; PR #839 CI green (56 pass/1 skip/0 fail; 0 GHAS). Note: `validate_distro_configs.sh` has a pre-existing rc=1 on the `NFTBAN_LOGIN_*` line-format check that is unrelated to this lane (diff vs main empty). **Prior release lane:** v1.180.0 (2026-06-13) — **LoginMon FTP AUTH-INPUT (FTP-only): FTP authentication failures owned by LoginMon `auth_failure`.** (PR [#837](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/837), sq `999c9428`; record `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_180_LOGINMON_FTP_IMPL_RECORD.md`.) **What:** the Go `FTPDetector` existed but was unfed, and — the fleet-wide blind spot — production **pure-ftpd logs auth failures to syslog facility ftp (11)**, which the LoginMon journal watcher did not consume (auth/authpriv only); `/var/log/pureftpd.log` is stats-only/empty and is NOT an auth source. v1.180 wires two source paths matched to how each daemon logs: **pure-ftpd → journal `SYSLOG_FACILITY=11`** (added to `runJournalWatcher`; consumed via `processLine`) and **vsftpd/proftpd → file watchers** (`internal/loginmon/ftpdiscovery.go`; pure-ftpd dropped from file globs to avoid double-counting). Owns ONLY the **`auth_failure`** event class (`ReasonFTPAuthFail`); no BotScan/BotGuard ownership of FTP auth. Root daemon (`CAP_DAC_OVERRIDE`) reads directly — no collector/spool. Zero-input health-visible (`[LOGINMON] ftpauth: state=OK|WARN_NO_LOGS|NO_LOGS`; pure-ftpd present → OK even with files=0; file-daemon missing file → WARN_NO_LOGS). **Fixed via real-prod sampling of srv1-4:** vsftpd file-format gate (required `"vsftpd"` substring absent from `/var/log/vsftpd.log` — only `FAIL LOGIN: Client "<ip>"`), pure-ftpd real `(?@<ip>)` extraction (the trailing `[<ip>]` bracket is never emitted; brackets hold level + username), IPv4/IPv6 family parity (16/16 paths). **Real-prod sampling DISPROVED adding cphulkd/exim-reject** (cphulkd login_log duplicates cPanel access_log-401 ownership; exim rejectlog auth subset duplicates exim mainlog/MailDetector, and its unique lines are spam/RBL/HELO not auth) → those are NOT in this release; reclassified to v1.181 plan-only dead-key/duplicate-source guard (`V1_181_AUTH_SOURCE_DEAD_KEY_AND_DUPLICATE_GUARD_SCOPE.md`). **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` source moves loginmon-only (`36978621`→`cce992a1`); schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change.** **Validation:** detector matrix (real pure-ftpd format, IPv4+IPv6, journal-vs-file routing, success-login control ignored) + `go test -race ./...` + `go build ./...` green; PR #837 CI green (53 pass/1 skip/0 fail; 0 GHAS); **lab2 binary-swap PASS** — IPv4→v4 set + IPv6→v6 set kernel-banned (`nft list ruleset`), success-login not banned, SSH preserved, test IPs scrubbed via IPC. Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). **Prior release lane:** v1.179.0 (2026-06-13) — **LoginMon WEB-AUTH RUNTIME (v1.178-B/v1.179): access-log `auth_failure` — HTTP basic-auth 401 + WordPress wp-login failed-credential.** (PR [#835](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/835), sq `405a8c79`; record `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_179_LOGINMON_WEB_AUTH_IMPL_RECORD.md`; ownership `LOG_SOURCE_OWNERSHIP_AND_MODULE_BOUNDARY_AUDIT.md`.) **What:** the Go LoginMon module (root `nftband.service`) detected WordPress but had no web access-log watcher, and apache/nginx HTTP-auth was advertised with no consumer. v1.179 adds `internal/loginmon/detector/webauth.go` + `webdiscovery.go` + a supervised web-log watcher (v1.176 respawn) that OWNS only the **`auth_failure`** event class on shared web logs: **HTTP status 401** (generic apache/nginx → `http-auth`) + WordPress `POST /wp-login.php` status-200 failed-credential (→ `wordpress`). **403, xmlrpc, wp-login floods, scanner/probe paths, bad bots are NOT matched** — `web_abuse` stays BotScan→BotGuard (one event class, one ban owner — per the LOG_SOURCE_OWNERSHIP contract). cPanel `/login/` 401 stays PanelDetector-owned. **Structural access-log parse** (`parseRequestAndStatus`: HTTP status parsed as a field, never the bytes-sent size; method/path matched in the request line only) — fixes a real-traffic status-vs-size false-ban vector. **DA httpd/nginx mirror double-count fix** (prefer `/var/log/httpd/domains`; skip the mirrored nginx dir). **`REST_401_SCORING_DECISION = CONSERVATIVE_DEFAULT`:** wp-login score 20, generic/REST-401 score 8, scorer TempBan threshold 45 unchanged (incidental logged-out browser/plugin REST-401s don't ban quickly; sustained probing does). Runtime consumer = **root + CAP_DAC_OVERRIDE** → reads logs DIRECTLY (NOT a DAC read-authority lane; **no BotScan collector/spool**). Zero-input is health-visible (`[LOGINMON] webauth: state=OK|WARN_NO_LOGS|NO_LOGS`). **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` source moves loginmon-only (`48b82663`→`36978621`); schema 1.83.0 frozen; NO packaging/FHS/cmd change.** **Validation:** detector + discovery unit tests (incl. **real sanitized production traffic** from srv1-4/dns1-2 + size-collision + referer-false-match + DA-dedup) + full `go test ./...` green + `-race`; CI #835 green (0 GHAS); **real-prod-traffic read-only sampling** (caught the two real bugs above) + **final lab binary-swap PASS lab2 (generic) + lab4 (cPanel domlogs canonicalize)** — 401 & wp-login-200 → loginmon ban, 503/size-200, 200/size-401, xmlrpc, 403 → NOT banned, SSH preserved, no failed units. Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). **Prior release lane:** v1.178.0 (2026-06-13) — **BOTSCAN_READ_AUTHORITY (v1.178-A): read authority via a separate cap-scoped collector → nftban-readable spool.** (PR [#833](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/833), sq `e0ed237e`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/BOTSCAN_READ_AUTHORITY_SCOPE.md`; record `BOTSCAN_READ_AUTHORITY_IMPL_RECORD.md`.) **What:** v1.177 surfaced that the unprivileged `nftban-botscan.service` cannot read panel access logs (fleet-validated **8/9 DEGRADED**, two blocker shapes: DA `/var/log/httpd/domains` `drwx--x--- apache:root` parent-traversal + generic/Plesk `/var/log/{apache2,nginx}` `0640 root:adm` file-read). v1.178-A adds **`cli/sbin/nftban-botscan-collector`** on a SEPARATE hardened unit (`User=nftban` + `AmbientCapabilities=CAP_DAC_READ_SEARCH` only) that reads **allowlisted** access-log roots (realpath-canonicalize → revalidate inside allowlist → symlink-escape reject → regular-file-only → bounded max files/bytes → per-read audit) and writes an **`nftban:nftban 0640` spool** under `/run/nftban/botscan`; the scanner becomes **spool-first**. **Scanner stays `User=nftban`, capless, `NoNewPrivileges=true`, `RestrictSUIDSGID=true`**; collector `ReadWritePaths` is tight (spool + offset only, not all of `/run/nftban`). **Package-family validation PASS:** lab2 (ubuntu24.04 **DEB**) + lab4 (almalinux9 **RPM**) — collector binary + both units + tmpfiles (`/run/nftban/botscan` + `/var/lib/nftban/botscan-collector`) land; restore-staging includes the collector; Shape B recovered on lab2, Shape A recovered on lab4 (real cPanel `domlogs` symlink-chain canonicalized); both report **"OK (served via collector spool)"**; no failed nftban units; SSH/firewall preserved (`V178A_PACKAGED_INSTALL_LAB_VALIDATION.md`). **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` SOURCE byte-identical to v1.177.0 `48b82663…`** (local `-trimpath -buildvcs=false`; 0 `internal/installer` deps in daemon). **Packaged daemon binary hashes may differ because CI embeds VCS metadata (`buildvcs=auto`) — NOT a code change; do NOT claim packaged binary byte-identity.** Schema **1.83.0 frozen**. **Does NOT** fix Go LoginMon WordPress/web runtime, FTP/cphulkd/exim-reject/http-auth, or a global health input-axis; **NOT** fleet-rolled-out; **NO** fleet-wide xmlrpc-mitigation claim. **Prior release lane:** v1.177.0 (2026-06-12) — **HTTP_LOG_DISCOVERY_AND_BOTSCAN_PANEL_PATHS: BotScan panel access-log discovery + diagnostic/readability truth + claim correction.** Shell/data/test only (PR [#831](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/831), sq `e2453ddd`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_177_HTTP_LOG_DISCOVERY_AND_BOTSCAN_PANEL_PATHS_SCOPE.md`). **Fixed:** (1) **BotScan panel access-log discovery** — panel-aware multi-glob discovery (DirectAdmin/cPanel/Plesk/generic Apache·nginx·LiteSpeed) replacing the single-file `[[ -f ]]` lookup, plus a bounded incremental/offset reader, IPv4/IPv6, and a `BOTSCAN_LOG_PATHS` override (`cli/lib/nftban/lib/nftban_http_logs.sh`). (2) **Diagnostic/readability truth** — `nftban botscan logs --detect` + `botscan status` report a five-state verdict **OK / DEGRADED / WARN_NO_LOGS / NO_LOGS / UNKNOWN**, evaluated AS the service account (`User=nftban`) via `runuser` — **never the caller** (root-blindness guard); unreadable and UNKNOWN states are surfaced instead of a bare "No access log found". (3) **Claim correction** — false LoginMon/Roundcube/apache-auth runtime-coverage claims corrected. **Does NOT grant BotScan read authority** — `BOTSCAN_READ_AUTHORITY` remains **OPEN** (on DA/cPanel where panel logs are unreadable to `User=nftban`, BotScan now reports DEGRADED but enforcement stays blocked until a later read-authority lane). **Go LoginMon WordPress/web runtime authority remains deferred; FTP/cphulkd/exim-reject/http-auth remain separate input-authority lanes** (`DETECTION_INPUT_AUTHORITY_AUDIT.md`, `SENIOR_ARCHITECT_ROADMAP_INPUT_AUTHORITY.md`). **Does NOT restore BotScan enforcement fleet-wide; does NOT mitigate xmlrpc on DA/cPanel.** **Envelope: shell/data/test only — daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL `48b82663…`** · schema **1.83.0 frozen** · no Go/systemd/packaging/ACL/Polkit change. **Validation:** `http_log_discovery_v177_test.sh` 30/0 (panel matrix + exclusion + dedup + bounded scan + incremental/rotation/copytruncate + IPv4/IPv6 parse + service-account readability T13–T19 incl. root-blindness guard), CI-wired; `bash -n` + shellcheck `-S warning` clean; GHAS Semgrep ifs-tampering cleared via command-scoped IFS (`6f9bf9f1`); daemon byte-identity reproduced; CI #831 green (52 pass/2 skip/0 fail); post-merge main `e2453ddd`. Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). **Prior release lane:** v1.176.0 (2026-06-12) — **DAEMON RELIABILITY: loginmon watcher respawn + FSYNC-RESIDUAL state writes.** Pure-Go (PR [#829](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/829), sq `b3c8c82b`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_176_DAEMON_RELIABILITY_SCOPE.md`). **CLOSED:** (A) **LOGINMON-WATCHER-NO-RESPAWN** — the daemon `tail -F` log-watcher is now supervised: on child death while ctx is alive it respawns with bounded exponential backoff (1s→60s, reset after 30s healthy); clean shutdown via ctx-cancel does NOT respawn and leaks no `*exec.Cmd`. A killed watcher child no longer leaves a detection source dark until daemon restart. (B) **FSYNC-RESIDUAL F-1/F-2** — `internal/stats/set_counters.go` (set_counts.json) + `internal/rebuild/marker.go` (recovery marker) now write via `safety.SafeWriteFile` (temp + fsync + atomic rename), replacing `os.WriteFile(tmp)+Rename` with no Sync; perms 0640 preserved. A new CI guard (`fsync_residual_guard_v176_test.sh`) blocks any NEW unapproved hand-rolled temp+rename Go state writer outside `internal/safety`. **The 13 pre-existing temp+rename writers are explicitly baselined as separate tech debt — NOT claimed fixed.** **Envelope: daemon `cmd/nftband` hash MOVES (expected, code-only `65ac698d…`→`48b82663…`)** — attributable to `internal/loginmon` + `internal/stats`; the rebuild change lands in nftban-core/installer; schema **1.83.0 frozen**; no FHS/packaging/schema change. **Validation:** Go `-race` (watcher respawn-on-death + clean-shutdown-no-leak + real-tail kill→respawn; set_counters/marker durable round-trip + no temp residue) + the FSYNC CI guard (self-tested) wired into CI; `go build/vet/test -race` + gofmt + gosec green; CI #829 green (56 pass/1 skip/0 fail); post-merge main `b3c8c82b` green (non-blocking Dependabot aside); **lab-first PASS lab2 (DEB/Plesk) + lab4 (RPM/cPanel)** via reversible daemon binary-swap — kill→respawn with unchanged MainPID + no leak + clean shutdown, set_counts.json durable 0640 no-residue across restart, validate/fhs rc=0 (`V1_176_LAB_VALIDATION_RECORD.md`). Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). Next: tag/publish v1.176. **Remaining:** v1.177 HTTP_LOG_DISCOVERY_AND_BOTSCAN_PANEL_PATHS (scoped) · SEC/VX · SUPPORT-DIAG · UX-MSG-AUDIT · firewall-validate `RuntimeDirectory=` (deferred).

**Prior release lane:** v1.175.0 (2026-06-12) — **FHS lane: auditors + sid-stats + alert-throttle FHS closed; firewall-validate retained as accepted non-fatal security exception.** FHS-generator + shell + `internal/suricata/stats` (PR [#827](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/827), sq `94069c08`; D-1 correction `2e910850`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_175_FHS_LANE_SCOPE.md`). **CLOSED:** (A) **BUG-TMPFILES-AUDITORS** — `/var/lib/nftban/reports/auditors` `created_by tmpfiles→package` (RPM `%dir %attr(0770,root,nftban-auditor)`; DEB `nftban.dirs`+postinst → `root:nftban-auditor 0770`); removed from tmpfiles → auditors unsafe-transition / exit-73 eliminated (both families). (B) **FHS-SMELL-SIDSTATS** — suricata sid-stats `/etc/nftban/...`→`/var/lib/nftban/suricata/cache/sid-stats.json` (`cfg.DataDir`) + `migrateLegacySnapshot` on first start. (C) **ALERT-THROTTLE-FHS** — `nftban-alert@` throttle/diagnostics relocated to nftban-owned `/var/lib/nftban/alerts` (`0750 nftban:nftban`, new tmpfiles entry) + `cli/sbin/nftban-service-alert` repointed → throttling now persists on `User=nftban` hosts (root-cause fix deferred from v1.174 Item 3). **RETAINED — NOT closed — BUG-TMPFILES-FIREWALL-VALIDATE = ACCEPTED non-fatal SECURITY EXCEPTION:** `/run/nftban/firewall-validate` (`2750 root:nftban`) stays in tmpfiles — only the audited root service writes `last.json` (nftban group reads 0640); `nftban:nftban` would let a compromised daemon forge the independent validation result. D-1 (drop tmpfiles, unit `+ExecStartPre` sole creator) was **REJECTED — lab-proven reboot-latent `226/NAMESPACE`** on systemd 252+255 (ReadWritePaths binds at mount-namespace setup, before ExecStartPre). **Audit truth: do NOT claim BUG-TMPFILES fully closed or systemd-tmpfiles exit-73 fully eliminated** — `systemd-tmpfiles --create` may still exit 73 because of firewall-validate (accepted). **Envelope: FHS-generator + shell + `internal/suricata/stats` (in NO `cmd/` binary) — daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL** · schema 1.83.0 frozen. **Validation:** `fhs_lane_v175_test.sh` (T1 generic root-under-non-root guard — parser `IFS` bug fixed from vacuous→real + negative-tested; allowlists firewall-validate; T2–T7) + reverted `v131_2`/`v131_3` + `tmpfiles_zz_v139`, all green; `generate-fhs-outputs.sh --check` rc=0; CI #827 green; post-merge main `94069c08` green (non-blocking Dependabot bot aside); **lab-first Option-I PROOF lab2 (systemd 255) + lab4 (systemd 252)** — ExecStartPre-sole → 226/NAMESPACE, tmpfiles boot-creator → service rc=0. Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `--check` rc=0). **Prior release lane:** v1.174.0 (2026-06-12) — **install/health reliability: stale failed-unit classification + health OOM cap + alert bookkeeping non-fatal + user messaging.** Installer-Go + shell (PR [#825](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/825), sq `cb7fb5b8`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_174_INSTALL_HEALTH_RELIABILITY_SCOPE.md`). The installer's `failed_units_postinstall_ok` flagged DEGRADED on a **pre-existing** failed nftban unit (failed *before* the upgrade) with no install-window timestamp comparison — observed on dns2 (`nftban-health.service` OOM) + monitor (`nftban-alert@nftband.service.service` stale latch) even though live health passed (`V1_173_INSTALL_WARNINGS_CLASSIFICATION.md`). **Fix:** (1) classify failed units vs an install-window start — pre-existing + clean live `nftban health` → `WARN_PRE_EXISTING_RECOVERED` (non-fatal; `--repair`→COMMITTED); in-window/unknown-ts/unknown-or-unhealthy → DEGRADED (never recover a NEW failure); (1b) `parseLiveHealthClean` accepts `Overall OK|IDLE|PROTECTED` + `Findings: none [(N INFO hidden)]` so it fires on a protecting host; (1c) bounded `reset-failed` for ONLY the recovered units; (2) `nftban-health.service MemoryMax 128M→256M`; (3) `cli/sbin/nftban-service-alert` non-delivery bookkeeping (throttle/metric/diagnostics) best-effort, `send_email_alert` keeps its rc contract; (3b) `cmd_update.sh` surfaces an auto-recovered unit + reworded DEGRADED text (no "known transient on the exporter"). **Envelope: installer-Go + shell — daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.173.0** (does NOT import `internal/installer/validate`; SHA256 `65ac698d…` unchanged + 0 `cmd/nftband` files) · schema 1.83.0 frozen · FHS body byte-unchanged. **Validation:** Go `systemd_payload_v174_test.go` (classification matrix + never-recover boundary + parser OK/IDLE/PROTECTED/INFO + templated alert@ + 6 reset-failed cases) under `-race` + shell `health_journalctl_bounded_v174_test.sh` / `alert_bookkeeping_nonfatal_v174_test.sh` (6/0) / `update_msg_recovered_v174_test.sh` (8/0), all CI-wired; `go build/vet/staticcheck/gofmt` + `bash -n` + shellcheck `-S warning` + gosec green; CI #825 green; post-merge main `cb7fb5b8` green (non-blocking Dependabot bot aside); **lab-first PASS lab2 (DEB) + lab4 (RPM)** — recovered-path fires on real PROTECTED hosts, in-window stays DEGRADED, restored to baseline. **Does NOT fix (open lanes):** ALERT-THROTTLE-FHS (relocate throttle to nftban-owned dir — v1.175 FHS), SUPPORT-DIAG-FAILED-UNIT-COVERAGE (support-bundle gaps — diagnostics release), UX-MSG-AUDIT (broad misleading-message scan). **Prior release lane:** v1.173.0 (2026-06-11) — **§4.1 session-whitelist cross-language flock + executor fsync.** Installer-Go + shell lane (PR [#822](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/822), sq `0a85527c`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V173_SESSION_FLOCK_SCOPE.md`). `/etc/nftban/whitelist.d/00-session.conf` had **6 read-modify-write writers across two languages with no shared lock** → a concurrent `nftban update` (Go `AddSessionWhitelist`) and `nftban firewall whitelist-session add/remove/cleanup` (shell) could **lose an update** (atomic rename prevents torn reads, not lost updates). **Fix:** one shared advisory lock `/run/nftban/session_whitelist.lock` taken for the FULL read→modify→write — Go `syscall.Flock(LOCK_EX)` (`internal/installer/safety/session_whitelist.go` Add/Cleanup/Remove) and shell `flock(1)` (`cli/lib/nftban/cli/cmd_firewall.sh`) both flock(2) the same path → genuine cross-language interlock; separate lock file (data inode changes on rename, `blacklistd.go` rationale); + `RealExecutor.WriteFileAtomic` now `tmp.Sync()` before rename (FakeExecutor unchanged; dir-fsync omitted per F-3). **Envelope: installer-Go + shell — daemon `cmd/nftband` BYTE-IDENTICAL to v1.172.0** (reader-only; **Prior release lane:** v1.172.0 (2026-06-11) — **CLI-PIPEFAIL-ARITH full-class sweep.** Shell-only lane merging the former v1.172 (HIGH) + v1.173 (MED/LOW) into one release (PR [#820](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/820), sq `26a53867`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V172_CLI_PIPEFAIL_SWEEP_SCOPE.md`). Under `set -Eeuo pipefail` a `producer | <emit-on-empty> … || <fallback>` inside `$()` double-emits when an early pipe stage fails (zero-match `grep` rc=1, head-SIGPIPE rc=141, or `jq` parse error) while a later stage already printed a default → `"0\n0"` → consumer `[[ -eq/-gt ]]` / `jq tonumber` crash. **Fixed 16 sites** (proven v1.170 idioms: wrap producer / `head -1` / `jq -s 'add // [] | length'` + strip-and-default; dropped `|| echo X` → `|| true` for set-e safety): **HIGH** report_email empty-set counts + watchdog history; **MED** report_data, ddos (blocked+packets/bytes), stats (offenders/ip-total/countries), cleanup, login+update digest/history, **+3 siblings** the audit missed (login_alert, health fd_count, service_control timers_active — directly `[[ -eq 0 ]]`-consumed); **LOW** wizard re-`readonly` guard + stats_format dead `grep -c|grep -c` removal. **Envelope: shell-only — `0 cmd/nftband`+`internal` (daemon byte-identical to v1.171.0) · schema 1.83.0 frozen · FHS body byte-unchanged · no packaging/dependency change · hermetic (NO lab gate).** **Validation:** new guard `cli_pipefail_arith_sweep_v172_test.sh` 20/0 (behavioral single-emit + static lint, CI-wired); empirical set-e abort-probe on every patched idiom → graceful 0; regression v170 7/0 + MAC 17/0; `bash -n` 13/13 + shellcheck `-S warning` 0 new; daemon byte-identity reasserted; CI #820 green; post-merge main `26a53867` green (non-blocking Dependabot bot aside). Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` only; `generate-fhs-outputs.sh --check` rc=0). Next: tag/publish v1.172. **Remaining (post-v1.172 order):** §4.1 session-flock (v1.173) · FSYNC-RESIDUAL F-1/F-2 (v1.174) · FHS lane BUG-TMPFILES + sid-stats-under-/etc (v1.175) · SEC cluster / VX (v1.176) · fleet rollout (HOLD).

**Prior release lane:** v1.170.0 (2026-06-10) — **`stats ip` history fix: pipefail + rotated/compressed logs.** Closes **BUG-STATS-IP-HISTORY-PIPEFAIL** — `nftban stats ip <IP>` crashed with a bash arithmetic error + garbled output for any zero-ban-record IP (PR [#816](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/816), sq `5e6ddc94`; scope `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V170_STATS_IP_HISTORY_FIX_SCOPE.md`). Root cause: `nftban_stats_ip_history()` `grep|awk||echo "[]"` double-emitted `"[]\n[]"` under `set -Eeuo pipefail` on a zero-match grep → caller `jq length` `"0\n0"` → `cmd_stats.sh:924 [[ -eq ]]` crash. **Fix:** single-emit producer (capture matcher; awk emits valid `[]` on empty) + `cmd_stats.sh` sanitizes the total to one integer. **Plus:** history now reads rotated/compressed archives (`bans.log` + `bans.log.*`/`.gz` via `zgrep`, timestamp-sorted) so it isn't truncated to the current window; graceful-degrade to the live log + WARN if `zgrep`/`gzip` absent (never hard-error); `gzip` already a declared dep (DEB `Depends` + RPM `Requires`) — CI now asserts it. **Envelope: shell-only — `0 cmd/nftband`+`internal` (daemon byte-identical to v1.169.0) · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · no dependency mutation.** **Validation:** new guard `stats_ip_history_v170_test.sh` 7/0 (CI-wired) — zero-match→single `[]`/no arith crash, compressed-only `.gz` IP read, merged+sorted; `bash -n` + shellcheck `-S warning` (0 new); CI #816 green (52/0/2-skip); post-merge main `5e6ddc94` green (non-blocking Dependabot bot aside). **Prior release lane:** v1.168.0 (2026-06-09) — **CLI-BUG-2: whitelist TTLs expire in-kernel.** Closes **CLI-BUG-2** (HIGH/security): `nftban firewall whitelist-session add <ip> --ttl <dur>` reported an expiry but the entry never expired in the kernel — the daemon's `FullSync` repopulated the whitelist sets from an IP-only snapshot (`EXPIRES_AT` was load-time skip-only), clobbering any kernel timeout on the next sync/rebuild so a "temporary" whitelist became permanent. (PR [#812](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/812), sq `dd794d1a`.) **Fix in the runtime authority (loader → state → snapshot → sync):** loader carries the absolute `ExpiresAt` (`internal/whitelist/loader.go parseExpiresAt`); `IPEntry.ExpireAt` populated + new `GetWhitelistSnapshotWithExpiry()` (`internal/runtime/state.go`); pure `remainingTimeout()` + timeout-aware `SyncWhitelistSetToNFT`, with `FullSync` applying `AddIPWithTimeout(remaining)` **anchored to the absolute expiry** so a re-sync **refreshes** the remaining TTL rather than clobbering it to permanent (`internal/setsync/diff.go`); daemon passes the expiry snapshot into `FullSync` (`cmd/nftband/daemon_handlers_sync.go`). **Render prerequisite:** `whitelist_ipv4`/`whitelist_ipv6` declare `flags interval, timeout` in static `install/nftables/nftables.conf` + template `nftables.conf.tpl` (blacklist already had it). **Upgrade activation:** the installer's existing `switchop.Rebuild` (`nftban firewall rebuild`) re-renders the template and recreates the sets with the new flag on upgrade — validated to activate **without manual rebuild** on lab2/lab4/monitor; a conditional, SSH-guarded **backstop** in the DEB postinst + RPM `%post` (v1.145 PR-A.1 pattern) recreates a still-flagless set as defense-in-depth and was **dormant** during validation. **Envelope: daemon-Go + render + packaging + tests — the daemon `cmd/nftband` byte-identical chain (v1.147→v1.167; tree `85a2de3e…`) ENDS HERE INTENTIONALLY · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · FHS body byte-unchanged.** **Validation:** local `go build/test ./...` + vet + staticcheck + gofmt + schema-freeze + shell guards + shellcheck green; primary `TestRemainingTimeout_TTLSurvivesFullSync` + CI-wired `whitelist_set_timeout_flag_v168` + `whitelist_timeout_upgrade_activation_v168`; **kernel-level (binary-swap + real package upgrade) PASS on lab2 (DEB), lab4 (RPM/EL9), monitor (real host, v1.159→v1.168 jump)** — timed `--ttl` kept a shrinking kernel timeout across an extra FullSync, durable/trust permanent, SSH preserved, hosts restored; activation by **normal upgrade rebuild** (backstop dormant); dns1/dns2 untouched (record `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V168_CLI_BUG_2_VALIDATION_RECORD.md`). PR #812 CI green; post-merge main `dd794d1a` green (sole failure = non-blocking Dependabot Updates bot run). **Prior release lane:** v1.165.0 (2026-06-08) — **RPM packaging correctness + hidden spec-parser hardening.** Two static, build-time-only packaging fixes; no runtime/firewall change. **(PR-A)** generated-spec **comment-macro guard** (`cli/lib/nftban/tests/rpm_spec_no_section_macro_in_comment_v165_test.sh`, PR [#801](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/801), sq `888dc67c`) — closes the **hidden rpm-4.16 hazard** where a bare `%install` token inside a generated-spec **comment** is parsed as a *second* `%install` section (`error: second %install`) and fails Build RPM el9/el10 deterministically, while rpm 4.18 (lab2)/6.x are lenient and miss it (bit twice: v1.157 + v1.164 PR-C). Ground-truth reproduced on **srv2 rpm 4.16.1.3**: only `%install` is fatal-in-comment, but the guard forbids the full section-macro family defensively; 7 existing benign comments reworded; CI-wired **before** rpmbuild. **(PR-B)** lib-dir **RPM `%files` dedup** (PR [#802](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/802), sq `848ac6b1`) — the 12 generator-owned `/usr/lib/nftban` payload dirs converted from bare paths to `<dir>/*` so `%include nftban-files.inc` keeps **sole** `%dir` ownership (removes the benign "File listed twice" warning); a `%install` dotfile strip (`find %{buildroot}/usr/lib/nftban -name '.*' -type f -delete`) prevents the rpm `<dir>/*` glob from orphaning `tests/.gitkeep` (the exact v1.161 `/*` failure). Static dedup guard `rpm_files_no_double_dir_listing_v165_test.sh` added + CI-wired. **Explicitly NOT shipped (separate lanes):** **PR-C templates/FHS correction** (the `templates/email`+`partials` subdirs lack inc `%dir` → requires an FHS-generator change that **changes FHS body**; deferred to **v1.166** `OPEN_RPM_FILES_DEDUP_PR_C_SCOPE`) · schema changes · daemon-Go · runtime/firewall behavior. **Envelope: packaging-build-script + tests + CI only — `0 cmd/nftband` (daemon byte-identical to v1.164.0; chain v1.147→v1.164.0 holds; tree `85a2de3e…`) · `0 cmd/nftban-core` · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · no CSF · no FHS-generator/`nftban-files.inc`/`fhs-spec.yaml` change · FHS body byte-unchanged.** The shipped DEB/RPM packages change intentionally (cleaner `%files`). **Validation:** PR-A guard 5/0 + PR-B guard 15/0 + v157 guard 6/0 (hermetic); **hermetic mini-rpmbuild** proved no double-listing + no unpackaged `.gitkeep` (rpm-version-independent); CI #801 + #802 **Build RPM el9/el10 ✅**, Policy Gates / Semgrep / ShellCheck ✅; both post-merge mains green (Docker-Hub pull-timeout infra flakes rerun-cleared).
