**Prior release lane:** v1.164.0 (2026-06-08) — **switchop classify-content safety.** Closes the v1.161 reopened installer-correctness debts (§2.2 raw-ghost + refuse-on-populated `inet filter`) by making `internal/installer/switchop` cleanup **classify content before deleting** instead of deleting known table names on sight (PR [#799](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/799), sq `42183f78`). **Ships:** a shared `TableIsEmpty(exec, family, table)` helper (`internal/installer/switchop/classify.go`, generalizing the `detect/cve_inet_filter` rule-count classifier) wired into `CleanGhostTables` (`ghost.go`) in three classes — **(1)** unconditional compat-shim skeletons (filter/nat/mangle/security/firewalld) unchanged; **(2) empty `ip raw`/`ip6 raw` skeleton → removed, populated/safety-relevant raw (e.g. operator/kernel NOTRACK) → preserved + WARN**; **(3) `inet filter` empty skeleton → removed (CVE-2025-NFTBAN-001 guard), populated operator-owned → preserved + WARN/refuse** unless `NFTBAN_ALLOW_REMOVE_INET_FILTER=1` authorises a logged high-risk removal (aligns the Go cleanup path with the DEB/RPM scriptlets + the detect verdict). The **PR26.6 invariant test is refined**: empty `ip raw` is now correctly removed (it is an iptables-nft skeleton, not a kernel default in pure nftables) while populated raw + the operator/nftban/ssh_safety tables stay preserved. **Explicitly NOT shipped (remain open in register):** RPM `%files` double-listing cleanup (PR-C **reverted** — failed strict rpm 4.16 EL9/EL10 a second time, this round on a bare `%install` token in a generated-spec comment before `%files` was even evaluated; now a **dedicated micro-lane** that must first add a **generated-spec lint forbidding bare RPM section macros in comments** before `rpmbuild`) · daemon-Go lanes (§3.6 ssh_ports counter + v1.150 Lane-C) · SEC-AUDITLOG · SEC-DAEMON-PRIV · schema unfreeze · CSF restore. **Envelope: installer-Go (switchop) + tests only — `0 cmd/nftband` (daemon byte-identical to v1.163.0; chain v1.147→v1.163.0 holds; tree `85a2de3e…`) · `0 cmd/nftban-core` · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · no CSF · no packaging/`build_nftban.sh` change · no FHS-body change.** The installer binary (`cmd/nftban-installer`/`internal/installer/switchop`) **does** change — intentional. **Validation:** lab2 `go build` + `go test ./internal/installer/switchop/...` green (hermetic `MockExecutor.RunResults` empty-vs-populated cases + flipped PR26.6 invariant), gofmt clean, `cmd/nftband` tree `85a2de3e…` unchanged; **CI #799 — Build RPM el9/el10 ✅** (after the PR-C revert), `Build & Test` ✅, Semgrep/ShellCheck/Policy Gates ✅; post-merge main `42183f78` green (70/0 check-runs, full DEB+RPM matrix ✅). **Prior release lane:** v1.154.0 (2026-06-06) — **install timer-reload hardening.** Installer-Go only; self-heals the post-install systemd timer-wedge class. (PR [#779](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/779), sq `11ded868`.) **D-INSTALL-TIMER-RELOAD** — on the v1.142.0 fleet rollout one host (dns2) finished install with `nftban-unified-exporter.timer` stuck `active (elapsed)` + `Trigger: n/a` + 0 runs/24h (manual fix: `daemon-reload && restart`). New `internal/installer/services/timers_post_install.go` `RestartWedgedTimers` runs at the end of installer `phaseValidate` (after `SetImmutableFlags`, before `StateCommitted`): `daemon-reload` then restart **only wedged timers** (`ActiveState=active` with no next-elapse), **warn-only/non-fatal**, probe errors treated as healthy (zero false positives). 22-timer single-source-of-truth superset + `KnownTimers()` in `timers.go`; single call site in `cmd/nftban-installer/phases.go` covering all three `phaseValidate` terminal paths (incl. the VALIDATE_1-pass path where the wedge occurred). Tests: `timers_post_install_test.go` (mock wedge-recovery) + `timers_post_install_parity_test.go` (drift-parity `KnownTimers()` ≡ `install/systemd/*.timer`). **Envelope: installer-Go only — `cmd/nftband` daemon byte-identical to v1.153.0 (chain v1.147→v1.153.0 holds) · `0 cmd/nftban-core` · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · no CSF · no UX/shell spillover.** The installer binary (`cmd/nftban-installer`/`internal/installer`) **does** change — intentional; **first release in this train where installer byte-identity is NOT preserved** (daemon still is). **Validation (lab2 go1.25.11, rebased on v1.153.0 main `f1a92d3a`):** `go vet ./...` rc=0 · `go build ./...` rc=0 · `go test -race` PASS (internal/installer/services + cmd/nftban-installer) · `go mod tidy` clean. (PR [#777](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/777), sq `82572f91`; 6 commits A–F.) **(A) status output-truth + cross-command consistency** — `cmd_status.sh` authority block reworded so neutralized legacy firewalls no longer read as active conflicts (`🔒 EXCLUSIVE` / `🛡️ NEUTRALIZED:`), timers line `Core N/N active · Optional M disabled · Failed 0` (was a misleading `8 / 16`), modules tagged `(optional add-on)`, posture stated once (health roll-up relabelled diagnostics), shared `nftban_kv` label helper. **(B) banner discipline + universal suppression** — one banner path, `Cmd:` shows the real subcommand, full box restricted to version/status/first-run, `--no-banner`/`--plain`/`--quiet`/`NFTBAN_NO_BANNER` honored universally (`--json` unaffected). **(C) error-text normalization** — 3-line `ERROR/Hint/Run --help` form replaces the ~30-line usage reprint; EUID≠0 inline sudo hint. **(D) list IPv6 column clamp** — human `list all` table width-clamped; full range stays in `--json`. **(E) installer-output wording (wording only)** — named WARN classes (`WARN_PERMISSIONS_ENFORCE_NONFATAL`, `WARN_TMPFILES_73`) in the editable `nftban_health_fixes.sh` (not the gate-frozen generated `nftban_fhs_spec.sh`); `panelfw.go` port-range compress; `main.go` "completed with warnings (non-fatal)". **(F) short-help verb icons (UX-A7) + output-truth wording sweep.** **Envelope: shell + tests + installer-Go wording only — `0 cmd/nftband` (daemon byte-identical to v1.152.0) · `0 cmd/nftban-core` · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · `0` VERSION-drift · no direct nft · no CSF.** **Tests:** 6 new suites `status_consistency_v153` 15/0 · `banner_nobanner_v153` 16/0 · `error_text_3line_v153` 12/0 · `list_ipv6_clamp_v153` 10/0 · `installer_wording_v153` 13/0 · `help_verb_icons_v153` 22/0; 10 regression guards green; shellcheck 0.9.0 rc=0; Go lab2 (go1.25.11) mod-tidy/vet/build/`-race` PASS (panelfw + adapters + installer); `check-nft-writes` 0. **Prior release lane:** v1.152.0 (2026-06-06) — **feeds + stats truth cleanup.** User-visible CLI/data-truth fixes; no functional cutover. (PR [#775](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/775), sq `496168bf`.) **(1) feeds-select tokenization under strict IFS** (`BUG-FEEDS-SELECT-NONFUNCTIONAL`, HIGH) — `strict.sh` sets `IFS=$'\n\t'` (no space), so `cmd_feeds.sh:231` collapsed multi-number/comma input into one un-parseable token → "No valid feeds selected" for every multi/comma selection (single number survived). Fix: `IFS=$' \t\n' read -ra parts <<< "${selection//,/ }"` (preserves tab/newline too). Root-caused via lab4 live trace; the prior CI stub ran without strict-mode IFS so it stayed green while broken. **(2) stats producer/consumer reconcile** (`13.10`) — the unified-exporter legacy branch read the always-absent `.permanent`/`.temporary` keys → `perm=0,temp=0` while total=active, so `nftban stats` showed `5 (perm:0 temp:0 manual:2)`. Now all-as-permanent like the daemon-cache branch so **total = perm + temp** on every path; `manual` is a labelled **subset** (`incl. manual`), not additive (daemon perm/temp split deferred to Lane C). **(3) status DOWN rc-contract** (`BUG-S1a/S1b`) — the two ERROR+DOWN paths in `_nftban_protection_state_validator` returned rc=0 (bare `return`); now `return 1`, with the 3 plain `var=$(_nftban_protection_state)` captures guarded `|| true` so the DOWN sentinel is preserved and the top-level exit stays mapped from `base_state` (DOWN→2). **Envelope: shell + tests only — `0 cmd/nftband` (daemon byte-identical to v1.151.0) · `0 cmd/nftban-core` · `0 internal` Go · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · `0` VERSION-drift · no direct nft · no CSF.** **Tests:** `feeds_select_strict_e2e_v152_test.sh` 13/0 · `stats_producer_reconcile_v152_test.sh` 10/0 · `status_rc_contract_v152_test.sh` 7/0; shellcheck clean; `check-nft-writes` 0. **Deferred (recorded):** `BUG-CtCount-feeds` feed-counter unification (became a broader helper-refactor) + 6 other no-`IFS` `read -ra <<<` sites (separate read-only triage). Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header regen; FHS body byte-unchanged). **Prior release lane:** v1.151.0 (2026-06-06) — **small cleanup / trust-output hotfix.** Four operator-visible false/confusing-output fixes; no functional cutover. (PR [#773](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/773), sq `a6d57e89`.) **(1) panelfw weak-DA false-positive** — `internal/installer/panelfw/panelfw.go` now gates `finalizeDetected` on `Confidence != "weak"`, so a weak single-signal detection (e.g. a bare `:2222` alt-SSH listener on a no-panel host) is no longer validated and does **not** print the DirectAdmin port set as if confirmed; it logs "weak signal ignored". **(2) exporter rc=143 false-error class-killer** — `nftban_unified_exporter.sh:48` signal-aware ERR trap: `rc>=128` (SIGTERM/SIGINT) → graceful INFO ("interrupted by signal; next run will collect"), no `ERROR:`; `rc<128` keeps the loud diagnosable line (ends the v1.143.1 per-site whack-a-mole). **(3) build-date truth** — `lib/version.sh` no longer prints the current clock as "Build Date"; resolves build-stamp file → `rpm` BUILDTIME → `"unknown"`, never `$(date)`. **(4) rebuild-log truth** — `internal/installer/switchop/rebuild.go` logs a real degraded reason (stderr → last stdout line → static) and never the self-contradictory "completed (exit 1)". **Envelope: `0 cmd/nftband` (daemon byte-identical to v1.150.1) · `0 cmd/nftban-core` · `0` schema (1.83.0 frozen) · no direct nft · no CSF;** installer-Go limited to panelfw + rebuild-log (output/decision wording only); exporter + build-date are shell. **Tests:** Go (lab2 go1.25) `gofmt`/`vet`/`build` clean + `panelfw_test.go` +2 + `rebuild_test.go` +3 PASS; shell `exporter_sigterm_graceful_v151_test.sh` 11/0 + `version_build_date_v151_test.sh` 8/0; `check-nft-writes.sh` 0 WRITE violations. **Prior release lane:** v1.150.1 (2026-06-05) — **external admin SSH-port guard (warn-only + lockout-net).** A small, lockout-adjacent hotfix on top of v1.150.0 for the `OBS-SSHPORT-55000-FAMILY` host-config class (srv1, dns2): hosts where admin SSH arrives on an **external** redirect/NAT port (e.g. `:55000 → :22` via firewalld / iptables-nft / provider / panel) can have that port transiently dropped by `nftban firewall rebuild`/`takeover`, which disarms competing firewall layers — `sshd` listens only on `:22`, so nftban correctly renders `ssh_ports = { 22 }` and has no detection source that can see `:55000` (no conntrack). `:22` is never at risk. **Fix** (PR [#769](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/769), sq `dbf7d8f3`; strategy `WARN_ONLY_PLUS_LOCKOUT_NET`): a new read-only `nftban firewall ssh-audit` report (sshd listeners vs `ssh_ports` vs declared `NFTBAN_EXTERNAL_ADMIN_SSH_PORTS`), and a pre-`rebuild`/`reload`/`takeover` guard that **warns** on the mismatch and **session-whitelists the active admin source IP via the existing IPC `whitelist-session` path** (lockout-net — SSH survives by IP regardless of port; recursion-guarded; opt-out `NFTBAN_NO_PREREBUILD_LOCKOUT=1`; TTL `NFTBAN_PREREBUILD_LOCKOUT_TTL` default 1h). **Rejected by design:** importing `:55000` into `ssh_ports` (it drives the brute-force rate-limit on the real sshd *listener*; sshd is not on `:55000`) and recreating/preserving the external NAT/redirect (nftban is an ingress IPS, not a NAT manager — the redirect stays host-managed). **Shell + CI + docs only — `0 cmd/nftband`, `0 cmd/nftban-core`, `0 internal` Go, `0` schema, no direct nft writes** (lockout-net uses the sanctioned IPC path only). **Daemon byte-identical to v1.150.0**; **schema 1.83.0 frozen.** New 36/36 hermetic test (`ssh_admin_port_guard_v150_test.sh`) wired as a CI step; `check-nft-writes.sh` 0 WRITE violations; merged CI-green (Policy Gates, Shell Quality, ShellCheck, Runtime Truth almalinux-9 + ubuntu-24.04, all DEB+RPM build+install matrices, CodeQL/Semgrep/OSV/docs). Class is **pre-existing host-config debt, NOT a v1.150 regression** (behaves identically v1.142↔v1.150). New operator doc `docs/SSH-EXTERNAL-ADMIN-PORT.md` (plain-English root cause + verbatim CLI text, asserted by the test). Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` regen; FHS body byte-unchanged). **Deploy target:** srv1 + dns2 first, then resume the remaining fleet rollout on v1.150.1.

**Prior release lane:** v1.150.0 (2026-06-05) — **CLI-health truth & cleanup + nft-writer single-authority tightening + `stats --json` fix + manual-count cache truth (IPv4/IPv6 symmetric).** A truth-focused release: every change makes the CLI report what the firewall is actually doing, with **zero daemon change**. **Lane A — CLI-health truth + cleanup** (PR [#764](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/764), sq `bd2d35e5`): `stats`/`status` now report real ban counts (manual + auto-detect `blacklist_manual` no longer shown as 0; GeoBan state read from `geoban.d` instead of a phantom `grep "BLOCKED"`); RBL no longer reports false-CLEAN on resolver timeout/error or `host`-only hosts (dig/nslookup fallback + real rc capture); two unbounded logs (`security-audit.log`, `portscan-events.log`) gain logrotate + inventory coverage; `health verify` now flags a *failed* required unit instead of passing it as OK; the `set -u` unbound-var class (16.x) is fixed across `nftban update`-adjacent paths; the new geoban-refresh timer ships in DEB+RPM (systemd install-list + `docs/systemd/UNITS.md` + generated `%preun`/`prerm` cleanup-snippet parity); stale Fail2Ban text removed. **AUTH — nft-writer single-authority tightening** (PR [#765](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/765), sq `c3ee98d7`): the DDoS-classic penalty-escalation timer write now routes through the daemon IPC (`nft_ipc_add_element`) instead of a direct `nft add element` (single-writer authority; gated `NFTBAN_EMERGENCY_MODE` fallback, default off); the `check-nft-writes.sh` CI guard scan is widened to `scripts/`, extensionless `cli/sbin/*`, and `internal/` Go, with the allowlist annotated + pruned; adds `docs/ARCHITECTURE-NFT-POLICY.md`. **F2 — `nftban stats --json`** (PR [#766](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/766), sq `255bb757`): `nftban_stats_top_sources` no longer emits a trailing comma when some source counts are 0, so `stats --json` is valid JSON again (was failing `jq --argjson`). **F3 — manual-count cache truth, IPv4/IPv6 symmetric** (PR [#767](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/767), sq `3c792944`): the unified-stats-cache exporter now emits `blacklist_manual{ipv4,ipv6,total}` and the cache-hit dashboard surfaces it, so manual bans are visible on both cache-hit and cache-miss paths (daemon `watchdog.go` half deferred to Lane C). **Daemon byte-identical to v1.149.0** — zero `cmd/nftband` / `cmd/nftban-core` change across all four lanes (only `internal/nftbanconf/logs.go` log-inventory, dead-code-eliminated from the daemon). **Schema 1.83.0 frozen.** **Validation:** Lane A lab-validated DEB (lab2 Ubuntu 24.04) + RPM (lab4 AlmaLinux 9.8) — package builds, v150 hermetic tests 5/5 on both OSes, logrotate + `logs.go` drift-test green, live systemd checks (geoban-refresh installed-but-disabled, rollback.timer stays static after enable-all, watchdog trend populates, GeoBan status DISABLED→ACTIVE); AUTH/F2/F3 hermetic-tested; all four PRs CI-green (Policy Gates incl. the widened nft-writer guard, Build & Test, Runtime Truth EL9+U24, ShellCheck). Release-prep touches only `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` regen; FHS body byte-unchanged). **Prior lane:** v1.149.0 (2026-06-05) — operational hardening: whitelist `--static` durability + portscan false-ban corroboration + 2 urgent bug fixes (daemon byte-identical to v1.148.0; schema 1.83.0 frozen).

**Prior release lane:** v1.143.1 (2026-06-01) — **RC-AUDIT-2 Phase 3 exporter SIGTERM race fixes (patch release on top of v1.143.0)**. Single implementation PR ships; closes two new ERR-trap-pinpointed exporter SIGTERM (rc=143) race sites surfaced during the v1.142.0 fleet rollout. **PR-Phase-3 [#738](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/738) (sq `5d72a9af`) — `nftban_unified_exporter_collect.sh` two-site SIGTERM-race close.** **Site A** (srv1 CentOS Stream 10): legacy-kernel `.botguard.*` jq pipeline at `collect.sh:244` — added validity gate `if echo "$counts_json" | jq -e '.botguard'` ONCE before reading the six per-counter values (bg_suspect/pending/allow/grey/ban/emergency), then `2>/dev/null) || bg_X=0` belt-and-suspenders on each individual jq invocation; locals initialized to 0 at line 234 stay safe if the validity gate fails OR any individual jq is SIGTERM-killed. **Site B** (ub2604 Ubuntu 26.04 LTS): `systemctl show nftband.service -p ActiveEnterTimestamp --value` at `collect.sh:89` — wrapped with `timeout 2s` (~40× p99 healthy systemd-show latency). Pre-v1.143.1 the bare `|| echo ""` fallback only fired on non-zero exit; SIGTERM mid-systemctl-show kills the bash subshell BEFORE `||` evaluates — failure is process-termination, not non-zero exit. `timeout 2s` caps the dbus query so the bash subshell can land cleanly on `start_time=""`. Both fixes mirror the v1.136 Phase 2 surgical pattern at `collect.sh:642` (set_counts.json) — three-step pattern: safe default first, validity gate ONCE, belt-and-suspenders on each read. New test `cli_exporter_exit2_phase_3_test.sh` (37 PASS / 0 FAIL) uses stubbed-callable mirror with PATH-shadow `systemctl` stub (bash function override invisible to `timeout` which spawns via execvp); T2-B3 elapsed=2011ms proves the 2s bound actually fires against a 4-second sleep stub (vs. an earlier 15ms false-positive caught during test iteration). T-DRIFT row asserts both PR-Phase-3 markers + the v1.136 Phase 2 `:642` marker still present (regression guard — the prior fix was NOT disturbed). **Daemon byte-identical to v1.143.0** — zero `.go` touched. **The five-release zero-Go chain `v1.140.0 → v1.141.0 → v1.142.0 → v1.143.0 → v1.143.1` holds**: `cmd/nftban-core` + `cmd/nftband` SHA256-identical across all five releases; `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` body SHA256 `4e618bd7d4ea379496f6052d3215ce7602e9001ea66f6948c1952c8111d1f242` unchanged through the same chain (header `meta:version` regen only). Schema 1.83.0 frozen. Zero metrics labels added or renamed, zero portal API change, zero `build/fhs-spec.yaml` body change, zero packaging / systemd / install / build changes. **Forbidden surfaces honored** per scope `V1_143_EXPORTER_EXIT2_PHASE_3_SCOPE.md §4.3`: 0 ERR-trap removal (the trap is doing what it was designed for — every Phase-3 pinpoint is a victory); 0 blanket `2>/dev/null` broadening (only the specific per-jq / per-systemctl pipes get the belt-and-suspenders); 0 `set -Eeuo pipefail` weakening; 0 exporter auxiliary-classification change (stays `IsAuxiliaryUnit=true` so installs reach COMMITTED on persistent exporter aborts); 0 v1.136 Phase 2 `:642` refactor (regression guard asserts marker still present). Cumulative test evidence at PR-Phase-3 merge time: **253 PASS / 0 FAIL** across 18 hermetic suites (new in v1.143.1: `cli_exporter_exit2_phase_3` 37/0; preserved regression: v1.143 PR-A/B 49/0 + v1.142 PR-FS / PR-UX-C6 42/0 + v1.141 PR-A/B/C 115/0 + v1.139.2 `rollback_help_guard` 10/0). **PR CI at merge:** PR #738 52 SUCCESS / 2 SKIPPED / 0 FAIL; **post-merge main CI on `5d72a9af`:** 24 SUCCESS / 1 SKIPPED / 0 FAIL — no Fuzz-flake this cycle. **Vehicle decision:** `SELECT_V1_143_EXPORTER_PHASE_3_VEHICLE = v1.143.1` (patch release — same precedent as v1.139.1 hostname-fallback + v1.139.2 CLI-safety hotfixes; narrow scope; ships fast; clear lab4-style follow-up release). **Operator decisions honored:** `OPEN_V1_143_EXPORTER_PHASE_3_IMPL = GO_IMPLEMENTATION_ONLY`; `SELECT_V1_143_EXPORTER_PHASE_3_SITE_A = single-validity-gate-plus-belt-and-suspenders`; `SELECT_V1_143_EXPORTER_PHASE_3_SITE_B = bounded-timeout`; `SELECT_V1_143_EXPORTER_PHASE_3_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 2`. **Post-publish verification path (separately gated, READ-ONLY)** — on srv1 + ub2604 run `journalctl -u nftban-unified-exporter -n 200 --no-pager | grep -cE 'aborted rc=143'`; expect 0 in the next 24-48h window. If a NEW ERR-trap pinpoint surfaces at a different file:line, that's `D-EXPORTER-EXIT2-PHASE-4` — a separate future lane, NOT a v1.143.1 patch. Non-goals carried forward to v1.144+: **D-NFTBAN-GUI-DOC-DRIFT** (text drift sweep); **D-INSTALL-TIMER-RELOAD** (defensive daemon-reload in installer Validate phase; v1.144+ install/update-hardening); UX-C2 wall-of-text validation errors; UX-C5 `--dry-run` parity; module-attribution; detection-coverage; SCHEMA-UNFREEZE fork (gates v1.144+ metrics expansion); RC-AUDIT-2 Phase 2 RBL batch (cmd_rbl 4 functions); Phase 2 HEURISTIC batch (audit classifier widening); the 56 inline EUID-check sites refactor; FHS-WIKI parallel docs lane (out-of-band). PARKED indefinitely unless reopened: CSF (any form), daemon User=root→nftban + SystemCallFilter + SEC-AUDITLOG + SEC-WL-VERIFY + SEC-IMMUT. Only allowed files touched in this release-prep PR: `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header `meta:version` regen only; FHS body byte-unchanged — SHA256 `4e618bd7d4ea379496f6052d3215ce7602e9001ea66f6948c1952c8111d1f242` unchanged from v1.141.0 + v1.142.0 + v1.143.0). **Prior release lane:** v1.143.0 (2026-05-29) — **RC-AUDIT-2 Phase 2 cleanup release on top of v1.142.0**. Two implementation PRs ship; CSF excluded; install/update boundary locked to permission-enforce-only per operator's `SELECT_V1_143_INSTALL_UPDATE_SCOPE` decision. **PR-A [#735](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/735) (sq `b8887e30`) — FS3-MUTATION rc-swallow paths.** Five FS3-class loop+success / unconditional-return-0 patterns closed in mutation-path shell functions: `cmd_feeds::nftban_cmd_feeds` (feeds enable category arm) — per-feed rc capture, `⚠️ Enabled N feed(s); M failed: <list>` to stderr on partial failure, ✅ Successfully marker only on full success; `cmd_port::nftban_port_allow_add` and `nftban_port_allow_remove` — IPC apply/revoke failure no longer swallowed, `_v143_rc=1` propagation, return $rc; `cmd_port::nftban_port_allow_flush` — per-set IPC loop with `_v143_failed=()` accumulator, `⚠ Config cleared but N kernel set(s) failed to flush: …` to stderr when non-empty, ✅ All flushed only on full success; `cmd_metrics::nftban_metrics_enable` — `_set_metrics_backend` rc captured + propagated, Prometheus Successfully marker only when config-write succeeded. Same FS3 family as v1.142 PR-FS `nftban_feeds_select`. 28 PASS / 0 FAIL on the new `cli_fs3_mutation_v143_test.sh`. **PR-B [#736](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/736) (sq `cc587c24`) — FS3-DA rc-swallow paths.** Two FS3-class fixes targeting the lab4 R-PERM-1 audit finding: `cmd_port::nftban_port_allow_directadmin` CloudFlare-whitelist arm — `nftban trust enable CLOUDFLARE && nftban trust update` rc captured into `_v143_da_rc`, failure printed to stderr with `DirectAdmin licensing REQUIRES this — operator action required` + manual-remediation hint, function-final `return $_v143_da_rc` replaces the prior unconditional `return 0` (licensing-critical failure was previously silent to rc); `cmd_permissions::nftban_permissions_cmd_enforce` wrapper rc-truth lock — the wrapper already returned `$result` truthfully (that's what produced the lab4 `permissions enforce failed (exit 1) — non-fatal` line at `cmd/nftban-installer/phases.go:573`), but the ❌ failure block went to stdout with no actionable advice; BUG-FS1 — `IFS=','` only-comma split vs space-advertised menu help replaced by `read -ra parts <<< "${selection//,/ }"` accepting comma AND/OR whitespace. **Prior release lane:** v1.139.2 (2026-05-28) — **CLI safety + truth-telling hotfix (single PR [#722](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/722), sq `d70c1b2e`).** Bundled fix for three production CLI defects of the same class — the binary lies to automation. **Daemon byte-identical to v1.139.1** (no Go touched). Schema 1.83.0 frozen. Shell-only. 23 files / +396 / −43. **(1) Rollback `--help` guard (SHIP-BLOCKER):** prior to v1.139.2, `nftban rollback --help` invoked a real rollback because the dispatcher called `_do_rollback` without parsing `--help`. A CLI-audit invocation tripped the bug during the v1.139.1 rollout and downgraded a canary host (lab2 v1.139.1 → v1.133.0). Defense-in-depth fix at `_do_rollback`'s entry in `cli/lib/nftban/cli/cmd_update_backup.sh:82` catches every caller, current and future. The 4 dispatch sites in `cli/lib/nftban/cli/cmd_update.sh` (top-level rollback alias + github/git/local sub-modes) now pass `"$@"` so the guard sees the args. New `_rollback_help()` helper renders proper usage. New hermetic test `cli/lib/nftban/tests/rollback_help_guard_v1_139_2_test.sh` — 10/10 PASS. **(2) CLI exit-code contract test + CI gate (C1 from UX review):** the independent UX Honest Cruel Judge Review of v1.139.1 reported four error paths returning rc=0 while printing ERROR text — the CLI lying to `nftban X \|\| alert`-style automation. Code-truth verification against the source tree at `main @ 3ba23da9` shows all four paths already return rc=1; the review's evidence likely reflects an older installed binary or a `/usr/sbin/nftban` wrapper. Rather than apply theater "fixes" to already-correct code, the hotfix adds a hermetic regression test (`cli/lib/nftban/tests/cli_error_rc_contract_v1_139_2_test.sh`, 10/10 PASS) that locks the current correct behavior as the tier-0 contract (`nftban bogus-subcommand` / `nftban ban` / `nftban ban 999.0.0.1` / `nftban update --dry-run` all rc≥1; `nftban version` / `nftban help` / `nftban rollback --help` rc=0), plus a new CI step in `ci-architecture.yml` that fails any future PR that regresses these paths. **(3) GUI retirement (cosmetic):** the Web GUI (`cmd_gui.sh`) was structurally retired in v1.100.1b ([PR #502](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/502) "GOTH cross-cutting prune") but 29 stale "Web GUI" / "for GUI" / "for API/GUI" references survived across 19 files. All rewritten to "scripts/API" or "metrics" or removed, including `cmd_wizard.sh` user-facing prompts + status banners (the GUI question is gone; only the Prometheus metrics exporter remains). `ENABLE_GUI=0` pinned for backward-compat with downstream `/etc/nftban/nftban.conf.local` consumers, with v1.139.2 retirement-marker comments citing PR #502; dead `WANT_GUI` variable removed (shellcheck SC2034 clean). `nftban_fhs_spec.sh:99` "TLS certificates for GUI/API" deliberately NOT touched — it's a generated file; would conflict with byte-unchanged-body contract — deferred to a separate fhs-spec source-side cleanup lane. **PR #722 CI: 56 / 2-skip / 0-fail.** Local pre-push proofs: 22/22 bash -n PASS on every modified .sh, both v1.139.2 tests 10/10 PASS, GUI grep-zero on modified files (2 intentional retention markers only), zero `.go` files in diff (daemon byte-identical confirmed), YAML parse clean on touched workflows. Out of scope (deferred to v1.140.x per UX review's P1/P2/P3 ranking): C2 wall-of-text validation errors, C3 cache/kernel drift remediation, C4 banner chrome restraint, C5 `--dry-run` flag parity, C6 inline-sudo guidance, broader audit of the other 405 `ERROR:` printer sites (separate lane), `nftban_fhs_spec.sh` source-side GUI cleanup (separate fhs-spec lane), Ubuntu 26 / v1.140 Tier-1 introduction (separate lane; lab-first; HARD_HOLD on tag pending 172.x lab validation). **Earlier release lane:** v1.139.1 (2026-05-28) — **hostname-fallback hotfix (single PR [#720](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/720), sq `b050f7ab`).** Fixes a **PRE-EXISTING distro-compat bug** (NOT a v1.139 regression — shipped unchanged in v1.137.0, v1.138.0, v1.139.0; git-blame `97b2c9297` 2026-02-04). The exporter's hostname fallback at `cli/lib/nftban/exporters/nftban_unified_exporter_collect.sh:1474` called the SAME `hostname` binary in both branches: `hostname=$(hostname -f 2>/dev/null || hostname)`. On hosts that ship no `hostname` binary in PATH — notably the minimal `centos-stream10` Docker base image used in CI's Fresh-install Namespace Guard — both branches failed `command not found` (rc=127) and `nftban-unified-exporter.service` aborted. Exporter is classified auxiliary at install_state (v1.135 `IsAuxiliaryUnit`) so installs reached COMMITTED on EL10 even when the exporter aborted. **Real-fleet production EL10 hosts (`srv1` CentOS Stream 10, `srv4` AlmaLinux 10.1) both have `hostname` present — in-production blast radius = zero. The CI failure was specifically the minimal Docker base image; this hotfix is defensive hardening, not an active-incident fix.** New 6-link chain: `hostname -f || hostname || hostnamectl --static || uname -n || cat /etc/hostname || echo unknown` — `hostnamectl --static` covers EL10 minimal (kept systemd; dropped `hostname`), `uname -n` is POSIX-always, `/etc/hostname` is the kernel-set nodename source, `echo unknown` is the bulletproof terminator. Behavior preserved on Debian/Ubuntu/EL9 where the first link still wins. New hermetic test `cli/lib/nftban/tests/hostname_fallback_v1_139_1_test.sh` (10 assertions; T1–T6 source-link, T7–T9 PATH-shadow runtime simulations, T10 regression guard). **Daemon byte-identical to v1.139.0** (no Go touched). Schema 1.83.0 frozen. 2 files changed (146 insertions, 1 deletion). Verified `V1_139_1_HOTFIX_VERIFY_PASS_DEB_AND_RPM` on lab2 (Ubuntu 24.04 DEB) + lab4 (AlmaLinux 9.8 EL9 RPM); also `V1_139_1_HOTFIX_UBUNTU26_VERIFY_PASS` end-to-end on Ubuntu 26.04 LTS via the CI-built DEB at `49.12.220.155` (host-side 16/16 install assertions + 10/10 hotfix test + exporter rc=0 twice + idempotency cycle PASS). PR #720 CI: 50 / 2-skip / 0-fail after one infrastructure-flake rerun on `Build NFTBan Packages` (artifact-upload intermediary 403 on `Build DEB (ubuntu22.04)` — same class as the documented container-runtime-125 flake; single `gh run rerun --failed` cleared; matches prior v1.137/v1.139 rerun precedents). Records `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_139_1_{HOSTNAME_FALLBACK_HOTFIX_RECORD,UBUNTU26_INSTALL_VERIFY_RECORD}.md`. Only allowed files touched in this release-prep PR: `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header-version regen only; FHS body byte-unchanged). Fleet rollout kit ready at `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/139_1_ROLLOUT/` (9 hosts: lab2, lab4, monitor, dns1, dns2, srv1, srv2, srv3, srv4 — scope-locked by operator; bot-runnable; preflight blocks until tag v1.139.1 published). No host contact during release-prep construction.
**Earlier release lane:** v1.139.0 (2026-05-28) — **FHS authority hardening; three PRs (PR-A FHS-TMPFILES-ZZ + PR-B FHS-UNGEN-LOGROTATE-CREATE + PR-C ATG formalization) shipped contiguously on top of v1.138.0. Schema 1.83.0 frozen; no metrics/portal change. Daemon byte-identical to v1.138.0 (no production Go touched across the three slices; only `build/fhs-spec.yaml` metadata flags + `build/generate-fhs-outputs.sh` two-pass yq extension + regenerated `install/systemd/tmpfiles.d/nftban.conf` + two new Go test files + one CLAUDE.md anchor line).** Closes the two real high-drift FHS authority residuals the v1.139 re-challenge identified. **(1) FHS-TMPFILES-ZZ — PR [#716](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/716) (sq `61558cfa`):** the packaged tmpfiles file `install/systemd/tmpfiles.d/nftban.conf` had 47 `d` create-if-missing directives and zero `z`/`Z` reconcile directives; the gap was at the generator level (the script hardcoded `d` prefix in all three yq projections). HYBRID closure: a new `tmpfiles_reconcile: "z"` field on each `created_by: tmpfiles` entry; the generator emits a sibling `z` (non-recursive, safe for log/data dirs whose contents have varied modes) per annotated entry via a two-pass yq projection (mikefarah-v4 compatible — the original one-pass jq-style `if/then/else/end` was rejected by mikefarah and caught by lab2; fixed and re-verified before merge). Operator-administered surfaces protected via path exclusion. 4 files (spec + generator + regenerated tmpfiles + new 10-assertion behavior test). Lab2+lab4 perm-reconcile scratch proof recorded. **(2) FHS-UNGEN-LOGROTATE-CREATE — PR [#717](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/717) (sq `7daacb04`):** the `install/config/{nftban,nftban-suricata}.logrotate` `create MODE OWNER GROUP` directives were hand-authored with no generator emitting them and no parity test asserting they match the FHS spec; a future spec change would silently leave the logrotate `create` lines pointing at the stale identity. Locked via a new Go parity test (`internal/nftbanconf/logs_logrotate_create_parity_test.go`, 3 functions) that asserts every `create` line in both packaged logrotate files matches the matching `file_permissions` entry flagged `logrotate_create: true` in `build/fhs-spec.yaml` via longest-prefix lookup (honoring `exclude`). 2 files. **No logrotate template change** (current effective modes already matched the spec). Daemon byte-identical. **(3) ATG formalization — PR [#718](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/718) (sq `6a7cc9c1`):** promoted the workspace `V107_FHS_AUTHORITY_GRAPH_DESIGN_CODE_GAP_CLOSURE.md` to canonical `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_139_FHS_AUTHORITY_GRAPH.md` with a formalization preamble + GAP-closure status table + compositional authority-lock summary; V107 replaced with a pointer stub. Repo-facing slice: 1 line added to `.claude/CLAUDE.md` under `## Additional Resources` anchoring at the canonical doc. **Compositional authority-lock on logrotate now complete (v1.137 + v1.139 combined):** `LogInventory()` (v1.137 B-12) locks WHICH/HOW-OFTEN/RETAIN; `logrotate_create: true` (v1.139 PR-B) locks `create MODE OWNER GROUP`; `tmpfiles_reconcile` annotated entries (v1.139 PR-A) lock the tmpfiles reconcile axis. A future operator change to any axis requires reciprocal change in the others, with CI proving it. **Validation:** PR #716 CI green (51 / 4-skip / 0-fail); PR #717 CI green (53 / 3-skip / 0-fail); PR #718 CI green (45 / 2-skip / 0-fail — docs-only); post-merge `main` green at `61558cfa`, `7daacb04`, and `6a7cc9c1` (the post-PR-C run hit the known `FuzzParseLogLine` `context deadline exceeded` infrastructure flake on first attempt — cleared by the standard `gh run rerun --failed` one-shot remediation, matching the v1.137 release-prep precedent; zero code change). Lab-verified DEB+RPM on lab2 + lab4 using production mikefarah yq v4.44.1 — `generate-fhs-outputs.sh --check` rc=0 on both; live perm-reconcile scratch proof on lab2 + lab4 confirmed `z` works under systemd 252+ (the lab record notes that modern systemd's `d` also reconciles, so `z` is now belt-and-suspenders + explicit-intent + backward-compat for older systemd; safety still preserved via path exclusion of operator surfaces). **Intentionally NOT in scope (DEFERRED, already CI-parity-bounded per re-challenge):** FHS-UNGEN (a) Go fallback perms in `internal/installer/fhs/{permissions.go, paths.go}` — already locked by `TestRequiredDirsYamlParity` + `TestRequiredDirsModesAndOwnersMatchTmpfiles`. FHS-UNGEN (b) RPM file-level `%attr` — directory `%dir %attr()` is fully generated; file-level entries are a stable ~30-line hand-authored surface. **Intentionally NOT in scope (still PARKED):** GAP 3 sysusers recipe-vs-execution; CSF-RESTORE-on-uninstall; daemon least-privilege (`User=root → nftban`); Registry-2 unification; firewall-integrity (v1.138 lane, already shipped). Records `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_139_{FHS_AUTHORITY_HARDENING_SCOPE,FHS_AUTHORITY_RECHALLENGE_RECORD,PR_A_TMPFILES_ZZ_VERIFY_RECORD,PR_B_LOGROTATE_CREATE_PARITY_VERIFY_RECORD,PR_C_ATG_FORMALIZATION_RECORD,FHS_AUTHORITY_GRAPH}.md`. **Earlier release lane:** v1.138.0 (2026-05-28) — **firewall integrity & bypass alerting; two PRs (SEC-RULEFP + SEC-BYPASS-ALERT) shipped contiguously on top of v1.137.0. Schema 1.83.0 frozen; no metrics/portal change; no Registry-2 refactor (PR-C remains intentionally not opened). Daemon NOT byte-identical (SEC-RULEFP adds `internal/rulefp` package + capture hook in `cmd/nftband/daemon_handlers_elements.go` + record-only reconcile finding in `daemon_reconciliation.go` + a new `nftban-core verify-rules` CLI; SEC-BYPASS-ALERT adds the `service-alerts.log` entry to `internal/nftbanconf/logs.go LogInventory()`).** Closes the two operator-flagged undetected-bypass gaps surfaced by the v1.136.1 fleet recon. **(1) SEC-RULEFP — PR [#712](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/712) (sq `df281c08`):** canonical nftables ruleset fingerprint — `Normalize(ruleset)` strips counters/handles/timers/dynamic-set elements; `Digest()` = sha256; baseline written atomically (0600) only after a trusted apply/rebuild; new `nftban-core verify-rules` (CLI + health + record-only periodic reconcile finding) detects rule-injection and chain-policy flip with exit codes OK/MISSING=0, MISMATCH=2, NFT_UNAVAILABLE=3. **Verify never auto-refreshes the baseline** (the SEC-RULEFP invariant; tested by `TestVerify_DoesNotRefreshBaseline`). 11 files; lab2/lab4 controlled live inject + new-chain flip + counter/timer/populated-set churn proof recorded. **(2) SEC-BYPASS-ALERT — PR [#713](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/713) (sq `12399c23`):** the existing 9-detector shell library (`nftban_firewall_conflicts.sh`) populates `NFTBAN_FIREWALL_CONFLICTS[]` + `NFTBAN_FIREWALL_SEVERITY` on every health-timer run; until now that signal was trapped in in-memory arrays. New thin emitter wires the data to the existing logrotate-managed `/var/log/nftban/service-alerts.log` — deterministic grep-testable `[FW-CONFLICT]` line, per-service flock-protected throttle (default 3600s; env override `NFTBAN_ALERT_THROTTLE_SECONDS`), severity-aware re-emit on WARNING→CRITICAL escalation, future-clock-skew clamp. Closes the v1.137 B-12 silent inventory-authority gap by adding `service-alerts.log` to `LogInventory()`. Third audit (`V1_138_PR_B_DUPLICATE_ALERT_LOGS_THIRD_AUDIT.md`) residuals R1–R7 closed in-slice — no debt. 4 files; `service_alerts_v138_test.sh` 27/0 PASS on lab2 + lab4. **Validation:** PR #712 CI green (55 / 1-skip / 0-fail), PR #713 CI green (54 / 1-skip / 0-fail); post-merge `main` green at `df281c08` (24/1-skip/0-fail) and at `12399c23` (24/1-skip/0-fail); lab-verified DEB+RPM (`gofmt`, `go vet`, `go test`, `staticcheck ./...`, `gosec -nosec ./...`, shellcheck, RPM still-builds, controlled live mismatch detection); the lab gate was corrected mid-lane to include `staticcheck` + `gosec -nosec` after PR-A surfaced SC2034 / SA4000 / G306 / G204 findings the original lab battery had missed. **Intentionally NOT in scope (parked as PR-C orthogonal cosmetic):** unifying banner + health on Registry-2 (`NFTBAN_CONFLICT_REGISTRY`); banner change; `conflict_cache` writer. **Intentionally NOT in scope (parked):** CSF-RESTORE-on-uninstall and daemon least-privilege (`User=root → nftban`) — both remain operator-PARKED. Records `NFTBAN_ROADMAP/V1_138_{FIREWALL_INTEGRITY_SCOPE,PR_A_RULESET_FINGERPRINT_VERIFY_RECORD,PR_B_BYPASS_ALERT_RECORD,PR_B_DUPLICATE_ALERT_LOGS_THIRD_AUDIT}.md`. Daemon NOT byte-identical (D-UXV-16/17 + panel touch `internal/escalation`/`internal/persistence`/`internal/nftbanconf`/installer; the log-durability portion alone was code-identical). `copytruncate` on the main stanza (B-05); `installer.log`/`update.log` covered (B-09); divergent health-fix fallback removed (B-01); `logrotate` made a hard DEB/RPM dependency; `internal/nftbanconf/logs.go` rebuilt as the canonical `LogInventory()` authority bridged to the templates by a Go drift-test (B-12); GEO-CLEANUP-001. **(2) D-UXV-16:** `escalation.parseBanEntry`/`CountRecentBans` now parse the canonical BLC-1 pipe `bans.log` (was the dead space-delimited format → returned 0 → repeat-offender→permanent escalation silently never fired; now counts BANNED rows). **(3) D-UXV-17:** `30-persistent-offenders.conf` converged on a single canonical writer (`persistence.PersistBan`) hardened with a stable sibling-lockfile flock — eliminates the unlocked-append-vs-rename clobber. **(4) nftban-panel group retirement:** the dormant panel authorization identity removed across fhs-spec/sysusers, polkit (`30-nftban-panel.rules` + `nftban-panelctl`), RPM/DEB packaging, payload + install assertions, health WARN+self-heal, and CI (D-NEW-11 KEEP note superseded — its `nftban-panelctl` consumer was uninvoked); the auditor read-only tier (`20-nftban-auditor.rules`) and the SEPARATE hosting-panel firewall-takeover subsystem (`--panel-auto-takeover`/`--no-panel`/`cmd_panel.sh`) are preserved (conflation trap). **PR [#710](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/710) (sq `b9fc3da4`).** **Validation:** every blocker challenged against real code (audits confirmed) + verified read-only on **lab2 (DEB) + lab4 (EL9 RPM)** — `go test ./...` green, `-race` clean, generator→`sysusers` byte-identical, DEB+RPM builds with panel files absent + `logrotate` Requires intact, log-durability shell 35/35; **Prior release lane:** v1.136.1 (2026-05-27) — **exporter exit-2 Phase 2: surgical guard for the pinned scale-cache read; shell-only, daemon byte-identical to v1.136.0.** Closes the exact intermittent `status=2/INVALIDARGUMENT` the v1.136.0 ERR trap pinned in production: monitor was upgraded to v1.136.0 (official checksum-verified asset) and the now-live ERR trap caught the failure on its first occurrence (run #73565) — `nftban_unified_exporter_collect.sh:642` `_global_scale=$(jq -r '.scale_mode // "NORMAL"' "$_scale_cache" 2>/dev/null)` reading `/run/nftban/set_counts.json` (daemon-written): a mid-write/truncated cache makes `jq` exit non-zero (the in-`jq` `// "NORMAL"` default never applies — parse failed first), the unguarded `$(…)` fails, and `set -Eeuo pipefail` aborted the whole run rc=2. **PR [#708](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/708) (sq `e5cb7395`) Phase 2**: a one-time `if jq -e . "$_scale_cache" >/dev/null 2>&1` validity gate now wraps **both** pinned reads (per-set line 638 + global line 642); on a transient invalid/truncated cache the exporter emits the safe default (global scale = NORMAL) and skips the per-set rows that cycle instead of aborting, with a belt-and-suspenders `|| _global_scale="NORMAL"` covering the rewrite-after-validate race. **Strict mode (`set -Eeuo pipefail`) and the v1.136.0 ERR trap are preserved; scoped to only the `set_counts.json` block — no broad sweep, no installer/`install_state`/schema/metrics-label/portal/service-timer-unit change; `cmd/nftband` + `cmd/nftban-core` byte-identical to v1.136.0; Schema 1.83.0 frozen; `nftban_fhs_spec.sh` header-version regen only (FHS body byte-unchanged).** **Validation:** PR #708 CI green (50 / 2-skip / 0-fail); post-merge `main` green at `e5cb7395`; new `exporter_exit2_phase2_scale_guard_v1361_test.sh` 13/0 (truncated/empty/missing-field → NORMAL with no non-zero exit under strict mode); Phase-1 `exporter_exit2_resilience_v136_test.sh` 15/0 intact; shellcheck clean. Shell-only release (daemon byte-identical) → standard package smoke applies. **Post-publish validation:** deploy v1.136.1 to monitor + bounded watch confirming no `nftban-unified-exporter` exit-2 recurrence (no abort on a truncated `set_counts.json`). Origin `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/V1_136_EXPORTER_EXIT2_PHASE2_SCOPE.md` + `V136_MONITOR_DEPLOY_AND_EXPORTER_BREADCRUMB_WATCH.md`. `v1.136.x` hotfix slot authorized for this fix only. **Prior release lane:** v1.136.0 (2026-05-27) — **exporter exit-2 resilience + diagnosability (Phase 1); shell-only, daemon byte-identical to v1.135.0.** Fixes the exporter's own intermittent `status=2/INVALIDARGUMENT` under systemd (a volatile-read returning 2 under `set -Eeuo pipefail`, silent in the journal) — a pre-existing observability defect (`nftban-unified-exporter` `meta:version=1.39.0`, predates v1.135; v1.135 already classified it auxiliary/non-fatal at the install_state layer so it never DEGRADED installs). Scope `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/V1_136_EXPORTER_EXIT2_RESILIENCE_SCOPE.md` (resilience + observability, **not** emergency remediation; `v1.135.x` hotfix slot stayed HOLD/UNAUTHORIZED). **PR [#706](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/706) (sq `7d0a7998`) Phase 1**: (1) `nftban_unified_exporter.sh` gains a **permanent `ERR` trap** placed *after* `set -Eeuo pipefail` (**strict mode preserved**) — it converts a silent strict-mode abort into a journal line (`rc` + `file:line` + function + failing `$BASH_COMMAND`) then **re-raises the rc** (does not swallow); `errtrace` (`-E`) propagates it into the sourced collect/export functions, so the next intermittent exit-2 **self-pins the exact failing command** for the Phase-2 surgical guard; (2) `nftban_exporter_json_compat.sh` adds `_read_stats_json_retry` — a bounded retry that accepts only fully-valid JSON, so a daemon mid-write truncation of `stats.json` is retried rather than dropping the legacy-JSON cycle (errexit-clean, never `exit 2`). **Per scope: NO blind broad guard sweep** — the unknown failing read waits for the ERR trap to self-pin in production. **No installer/`install_state`, schema/metrics-label, portal, or service/timer-unit change; `cmd/nftband` + `cmd/nftban-core` byte-identical to v1.135.0; Schema 1.83.0 frozen; `nftban_fhs_spec.sh` header-version regen only (FHS body byte-unchanged); `NFTBAN_EXPORTER_DEBUG` verbose-trace toggle deferred to a later release (design-only).** **Validation:** PR #706 CI green (50 / 2-skip / 0-fail); post-merge `main` green at `7d0a7998`; new `exporter_exit2_resilience_v136_test.sh` 15/0 (ERR-trap attribution + rc preserved under strict mode; `stats.json` retry valid/missing/truncated/recovers); shellcheck clean. Shell-only release (daemon byte-identical) → standard package smoke applies. **Post-release path (Phase 2 dependency):** v1.136.0 release → monitor upgraded → ERR trap live → next exporter exit-2 logs the exact command/line → `OPEN_V1_136_EXPORTER_EXIT2_PHASE2` surgical guard if still needed. Origin reports `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/EXPORTER_EXIT2_{TRIAGE_READONLY,MUTATING_REPRO}_REPORT.md`. **Prior release lane:** v1.135.0 (2026-05-27) — **install/update-lifecycle correctness: critical-timer assertion + install_state/FAILURE_REASON finalization (Phase 1) and update-lock cleanup + mixed-drift unblock + exporter-settle auxiliary classification (Phase 2). First release since v1.130/v1.131 to change installer Go (`internal/installer/…`) — the daemon/installer binary is NOT byte-identical to v1.134.0.** Scope `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/V135_INSTALL_UPDATE_LIFECYCLE_SCOPE.md`. **PR [#703](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/703) (sq `196dd03b`) Phase 1 — D-MAINTENANCE-TIMER-SILENT-ENABLE + install_state finalization**: a CRITICAL core timer (`nftban-maintenance.timer`) that fails to enable/start now drives `install_state` **DEGRADED** (new assertion `core_timers_active_or_scheduled_ok`) with the timer named in `FAILURE_REASON`, instead of latching COMMITTED on a silent enable-warning; `NFTBAN_RECONCILE_CORE_TIMERS=false` is honored as intentional (assertion Skips, stays COMMITTED); `state.Transition` gains an empty-reason backstop so no DEGRADED is ever reason-less. Adds `Executor.ServiceEnabled()`, `services.CriticalCoreTimers()`/exported `ShouldReconcile`, `validate.ValidateCriticalTimers`/`GatherTimerInputs`. **PR [#704](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/704) (sq `442cd580`) Phase 2 — three update-lifecycle lanes**: **2.1 D-UPDATE-LOCK-LEFT-BEHIND** — `_cmd_update_main` split into a lock wrapper + `_cmd_update_main_locked`; `/run/nftban/update.lock` is now released on every terminal update path (was left behind on exit — observed as a 2-day stale lock on dns2); `UPDATE_LOCK_FILE` derives from `NFTBAN_RUN_DIR`. **2.2 D-UPDATE-MIXED-DRIFT** — a clean-&-complete on-disk package-ownership probe (`rpm -qf`/`dpkg -S` owns *every* core binary) added as a third migration-clean unblock in `_detect_install_type`, so a genuinely migrated EL/DEB host with stale source-era history updates through instead of aborting `mixed` (a genuine source-over-package mix still stays `mixed` — no unsafe bypass). **2.3 D-EXPORTER-SETTLE-WINDOW** — failed auxiliary units (the unified exporter) are classified non-fatal (`IsAuxiliaryUnit` → new `FailedAuxiliaryUnits` bucket; `failed_units_postinstall_ok` only counts protection units) so a transient `nftban-unified-exporter` exit-2 no longer DEGRADES the install, with a bounded settle re-poll in the gatherer; `nftband`/`nftban-core`/tables/validator failures stay HARD. **Tests:** Go — `validate/timers_test.go`, `services/timers_critical_test.go`, `state/file_test.go`, `validate/systemd_payload_settle_test.go`; shell — `cmd_update_lock_cleanup_v135_test.sh` (9/0), `cmd_update_detection_v135_clean_ownership_test.sh` (10/0; v126 detector 20/0 intact). **Schema 1.83.0 frozen** (no new validator field / metric / Prometheus label / Status-JSON or install_state wire key / config key; install payload unchanged beyond the rebuilt binaries). **CI:** PR #703 green (66 / 1-skip / 0-fail — Go gates `Build & Test`/CodeQL-Go/Secure-Go exercised), PR #704 green (57 / 0-fail); post-merge `main` green at `196dd03b` + `442cd580`; lab2 (Go 1.25.0) `go build`+`go vet`+full `go test ./...` green for both phases, gofmt-clean. **Real-host package validation — all PASS, recorded under `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/`:** lab2 DEB closing gate on a live Plesk host (upgrade → COMMITTED, clearing a real stale exporter-DEGRADED; `mask nftban-maintenance.timer` → DEGRADED naming it → unmask+enable → COMMITTED; forced exporter → auxiliary non-fatal) = `V135_PACKAGE_INSTALL_CLOSING_GATE_LAB2_PASS`; **monitor** real DEB upgrade `v1.131.4 → v1.135.0` (a genuinely failing exporter at validate-time classified auxiliary/non-fatal in the wild; A/B lock proof — the identical `nftban update local` left a stale lock under v1.131.4 but NONE under v1.135.0; `install_type=deb` not `mixed`) = `V135_VALIDATE_MONITOR_UPGRADE_PASS`; **lab4** AlmaLinux 9.7 / RPM / cPanel — both the EL **upgrade** AND a genuine **clean reinstall** (`INSTALL_MODE=install`) reached COMMITTED with cPanel panel-survival validated, timer-mask→DEGRADED-naming-timer→recover→COMMITTED, exporter→auxiliary, no stale lock, SSH preserved across the uninstall firewall-down window = `V135_VALIDATE_LAB4_CLEAN_RPM_INSTALL_PASS`. Coverage spans **DEB + RPM × upgrade + clean-install × Plesk + cPanel + no-panel**. **Non-blocking follow-up (NOT a v1.135 regression — exporter script byte-unchanged):** `nftban-unified-exporter` exits 2 intermittently under systemd but exit-0 manually on monitor = pre-existing transient exporter flakiness, parked as a separate lane (v1.135 correctly tolerates it as auxiliary). **Prior release lane:** v1.131.4 (2026-05-26) — **installer DEGRADED-state correctness hotfix; supersedes v1.131.3.** Live fleet validation of v1.131.3 found that **every install latches `install_state=DEGRADED`** even though firewall protection + D13 work, because the D13 unit `nftban-firewall-validate.service` (`ExecStart=/usr/lib/nftban/helpers/firewall_validate_run.sh`, added #687/#689) referenced a path **missing from the installer's payload-inventory allow-list** → `systemd_payload_inventory_ok` flagged it "unknown". Root-caused on lab4 (already on 1.131.3, exporter healthy, still DEGRADED); the `nftban-unified-exporter` exit-2 blip is coincidental/self-healing and is **NOT** the latch (`AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/V131_3_DEGRADED_ROOT_CAUSE_CORRECTION.md`). **PR [#693](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/693) (sq `7d3eec96`)** fixes three coupled defects: **D-D13-PAYLOAD-INVENTORY-MISS** — adds `/usr/lib/nftban/helpers/firewall_validate_run.sh` to `defaultInventoryPaths()` (`internal/installer/validate/assertions.go`) so `systemd_payload_inventory_ok` passes and installs land COMMITTED; **D-INSTALL-STATE-BLANK-REASON** — `state.Transition()` now preserves the current failing-assertion reason for DEGRADED (was cleared by `applyTerminalHygiene`) so `FAILURE_REASON=` is populated and `report()` renders the `Issues:` line (COMMITTED still clears); **D-DEGRADED-REMEDIATION-CMD-BROKEN** — remediation no longer prints bare `nftban-installer --repair` (not on `$PATH` → `command not found`) and **never `sudo`** (polkit wording policy) — it uses the bare full path `/usr/lib/nftban/bin/nftban-installer --repair` across the Go installer `report()`, RPM `%post`, DEB `postinst`, and the `nftban update` CLI. 8 files (+84/−9); pre-merge lab2 (Go 1.25.0) `go build`/`go vet`/`go test -race` green, both new tests pass, no regression. **Package-install validation PENDING (the gate that closes this):** `EXECUTE_V1_131_4_PACKAGE_INSTALL_VALIDATION` must take a v1.131.3 host stuck `DEGRADED` to **`COMMITTED`** (`systemd_payload_inventory_ok` passes, no unknown ExecStart payload path, D13 still rc=0 + `^Validator Status:` + `last.json root:nftban 0640` + handoff dir `2750`, nftband active, ip/ip6 nftban tables present, update lock not left active). **Fleet rollout paused at 4/7 (monitor/dns1/dns2/srv2 on v1.131.3 — functionally healthy, cosmetically DEGRADED) until v1.131.4 validates.** Only allowed files touched in this release-prep PR: `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header-version regen only; FHS body byte-unchanged). **Prior release lane:** v1.131.3 (2026-05-26) — **D13 setgid group-ownership repair; supersedes v1.131.2.** v1.131.2 fixed the sandbox *write* (the handoff `last.json` is now created under `ProtectSystem=strict`) but live validation found the file landed `root:root 0640`, not `root:nftban`, so the nftban-group operator still saw banner-only. Root cause: the unit's `CapabilityBoundingSet=CAP_NET_ADMIN` drops `CAP_CHOWN` → the wrapper's `chgrp nftban` fails silently; and the handoff dir `0750` (not setgid) → the wrapper-written file inherits group `root`. **PR [#691](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/691) (sq `84460198`) — setgid fix (narrow, no CAP_CHOWN)**: the handoff dir mode is changed `0750` → **`2750` (setgid)** in both the tmpfiles entry (`d /run/nftban/firewall-validate 2750 root nftban -`, via `build/fhs-spec.yaml` regen) and the `+`-prefixed `ExecStartPre=+/usr/bin/install -d -o root -g nftban -m 2750 /run/nftban/firewall-validate`. The setgid bit makes the wrapper-written file inherit group `nftban` automatically — no `chgrp`/`CAP_CHOWN` needed; the wrapper's `chmod 0640` still works (owner chmod needs no capability) → file `root:nftban 0640` → nftban-group reads it → `^Validator Status:` renders. **No CAP_CHOWN added; `CapabilityBoundingSet=CAP_NET_ADMIN` only; `ProtectSystem=strict` kept; narrow `ReadWritePaths=/run/nftban/firewall-validate` kept (no broad form); wrapper logic unchanged; no pkexec; no systemd-journal/adm; no polkit change.** New `v131_3_d13_setgid_group_ownership_test.sh` (11/0 +1 SKIP) asserts the setgid dir + capability-faithful group inheritance (runs the wrapper under `systemd-run --property=CapabilityBoundingSet=CAP_NET_ADMIN` proving the file gets group `nftban` WITHOUT chgrp; SKIPs as non-root in CI — real proof is lab validation); operator-visibility uses exact `^Validator Status:` (loose `IDLE|PROTECTED` BANNED). **Schema 1.83.0 frozen.** **Validation:** local suites green (D13-setgid 11/0+1SKIP, D13-sandbox 15/0, D13-v131.1 17/0, V130 28/0); shellcheck + FHS `--check` clean. **Package-install validation PENDING (the gate that closes D13)** — `EXECUTE_V1_131_3_PACKAGE_INSTALL_VALIDATION` (lab2 DEB → lab4 RPM); pass: non-root `nftban`-group user gets rc=0 + stdout `^Validator Status:`, `/run/nftban/firewall-validate/last.json` = `root:nftban 0640`, `/run/nftban` stays `nftban:nftban`, root path renders. **Fleet: skip v1.131.0/.1/.2; install v1.131.3 only after both-family validation passes.** Only allowed files touched in this release-prep PR: `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header-version regen only). No host contact during release-prep construction.
**Prior release lane:** v1.131.2 (2026-05-26) — **D13 sandbox-write repair; superseded by v1.131.3 (file landed root:root, not root:nftban — group-ownership gap).** v1.131.1 added the `firewall_validate_run.sh` wrapper to hand off validator JSON via `/run/nftban/firewall-validate/last.json`, but live package validation (`V131_0_PACKAGE_INSTALL_VALIDATION_REPORT.md`) proved it **did not work under the real `nftban-firewall-validate.service`**: the unit's `ProtectSystem=strict` (no write allowance) makes `/run` read-only, so the wrapper's write failed (`ExecMainStatus=1`, `last.json` never created) and the nftban-group operator still saw banner-only output. (The v1.131.1 PASS was a false positive — a loose `IDLE|PROTECTED` grep matched a banner word, and the wrapper test ran with `NFTBAN_RUN_DIR=/tmp` OUTSIDE the sandbox.) **PR [#689](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/689) (sq `6ff6f605`) — Option C sandbox-write fix**: (1) tmpfiles entry (via `build/fhs-spec.yaml` regen) `d /run/nftban/firewall-validate 0750 root nftban -` creates the handoff dir with exact group-readable perms; (2) the unit gains a **narrow** `ReadWritePaths=/run/nftban/firewall-validate` while **keeping `ProtectSystem=strict`** (NOT broad `/run/nftban`, which would expose the daemon socket; NOT `RuntimeDirectory=nftban/...`, which would re-chown `/run/nftban` off `nftban:nftban` and break the daemon); (3) a `+`-prefixed `ExecStartPre=+/usr/bin/install -d -o root -g nftban -m 0750 /run/nftban/firewall-validate` runs un-sandboxed to guarantee the dir per-start (subdir-existence robustness, since `ReadWritePaths=<subdir>` needs the path to pre-exist); (4) wrapper `mkdir` made best-effort. v1.131.1 added the `firewall_validate_run.sh` wrapper to hand off validator JSON via `/run/nftban/firewall-validate/last.json`, but live package validation (`V131_0_PACKAGE_INSTALL_VALIDATION_REPORT.md`) proved it **did not work under the real `nftban-firewall-validate.service`**: the unit's `ProtectSystem=strict` (no write allowance) makes `/run` read-only, so the wrapper's write failed (`ExecMainStatus=1`, `last.json` never created) and the nftban-group operator still saw banner-only output. (The v1.131.1 PASS was a false positive — a loose `IDLE|PROTECTED` grep matched a banner word, and the wrapper test ran with `NFTBAN_RUN_DIR=/tmp` OUTSIDE the sandbox.) **PR [#689](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/689) (sq `6ff6f605`) — Option C sandbox-write fix**: (1) tmpfiles entry (via `build/fhs-spec.yaml` regen) `d /run/nftban/firewall-validate 0750 root nftban -` creates the handoff dir with exact group-readable perms; (2) the unit gains a **narrow** `ReadWritePaths=/run/nftban/firewall-validate` while **keeping `ProtectSystem=strict`** (NOT broad `/run/nftban`, which would expose the daemon socket; NOT `RuntimeDirectory=nftban/...`, which would re-chown `/run/nftban` off `nftban:nftban` and break the daemon); (3) a `+`-prefixed `ExecStartPre=+/usr/bin/install -d -o root -g nftban -m 0750 /run/nftban/firewall-validate` runs un-sandboxed to guarantee the dir per-start (subdir-existence robustness, since `ReadWritePaths=<subdir>` needs the path to pre-exist); (4) wrapper `mkdir` made best-effort. New `v131_2_d13_sandbox_write_repair_test.sh` adds a **sandbox-aware** assertion (runs the wrapper under an actual `systemd-run --property=ProtectSystem=strict` with/without `ReadWritePaths`, proving the file IS/IS-NOT created — the class that would have caught the v1.131.1 miss; SKIPs as non-root in CI, real proof is lab validation); operator-visibility checks use exact `^Validator Status:` (loose `IDLE|PROTECTED` grep BANNED). **No pkexec; nftban group NOT added to systemd-journal/adm; no polkit rule or path change.** **Schema 1.83.0 frozen.** **Validation:** local suites green (D13-sandbox 15/0 +1 SKIP, D13-v131.1 17/0, V130 28/0, V131-sweep 6/0, V129 17/0); shellcheck clean; FHS `--check` clean. **Package-install validation PENDING (the gate that matters)** — `EXECUTE_V1_131_2_PACKAGE_INSTALL_VALIDATION` (lab2 DEB → lab4 RPM); pass: non-root `nftban`-group user gets rc=0 + stdout `^Validator Status:`, `/run/nftban/firewall-validate/last.json` = `root:nftban 0640`, `/run/nftban` stays `nftban:nftban`, root path still renders. **Fleet: skip v1.131.0 AND v1.131.1; install v1.131.2 only after both-family validation passes.** Only allowed files touched in this release-prep PR: `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` (header-version regen only; FHS body byte-unchanged). **Prior release lane:** v1.131.1 (2026-05-26) — **D13 hotfix on v1.131.0 (INEFFECTIVE — superseded by v1.131.2 PR #689; the wrapper could not write under the unit's ProtectSystem=strict).** v1.131.0's Option C path (`nftban firewall validate` via the polkit-authorized `nftban-firewall-validate.service`) was incomplete after real package install: the service ran and emitted validator JSON, but the CLI read it from `journalctl`, which an `nftban`-group operator cannot read (not a `systemd-journal`/`adm` member) — so the target non-root operator saw rc=0 with **banner-only** output. Confirmed cross-family (lab2 DEB + lab4 RPM) in `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/V131_0_PACKAGE_INSTALL_VALIDATION_REPORT.md`; **NOT** a DEB/RPM path-drift (FHS layout is identical across families; only the polkit-rule directory differs by distro standard, `D13_FHS_PARITY_NOTE`). **PR [#687](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/687) (sq `76b181a7`) — D13 Option C output capture**: new `cli/lib/nftban/helpers/firewall_validate_run.sh` wrapper (the unit `ExecStart` target) runs `nftban-validate --json` as root+`CAP_NET_ADMIN`, then writes the JSON to a group-readable file `/run/nftban/firewall-validate/last.json` (owner `root:nftban`, mode `0640`, pre-run stale cleanup, validator exit code preserved, JSON also echoed to stdout for the root/audit journal copy); `cmd_firewall.sh` now reads that file as the primary source (journalctl demoted to a last-resort root fallback only). **No pkexec; nftban group NOT added to systemd-journal/adm; no polkit rule or path change** (family-specific polkit install paths preserved — DEB `/usr/share/polkit-1/rules.d/`, RPM `/etc/polkit-1/rules.d/`). **Prior release lane:** v1.131.0 (2026-05-25) — **post-v1.129 defect-fix bundle** carrying four PRs merged to `main` after the `v1.129.0` tag. **v1.130.0 was never tagged** — the work labeled "V130" in the lifecycle docs (`#682`, `#683`) and the work labeled "V131" (`#684`, `#685`) are folded into this single `v1.131.0` release; there is no separate `v1.130.0` GitHub release or git tag. Scope files under `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/` (`V130_*`, `V131_*`). **PR [#682](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/682) (sq `090d73b3`) V130 PR-A — D6.A polkit Option C**: adds a new `nftban-firewall-validate.service` oneshot systemd unit (read-only nftables validation holding `CAP_NET_ADMIN`), a new `allowedUnits[]` entry in `packaging/polkit-1/rules.d/10-nftban-systemd.rules` so `nftban`-group operators can trigger it without root, `cmd_firewall.sh` routing through the unit, and the `internal/validator/validator.go` D6.B remediation text now referencing the (now real) service. systemd install-list + maintainer-script cleanup regenerated. **This is the FIRST release since v1.127.0 to change the install payload + polkit surface — it is NOT a no-op packaging release; it ships a new systemd unit and a new polkit rule entry.** 28 deterministic tests. **PR [#683](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/683) (sq `c543fc3a`) V130 PR-A.1 — D10 + D11**: D10 `cmd_firewall.sh` journalctl read now uses a bounded retry loop (journald async-write race; operator previously saw rc=0 with empty stdout); D11 `StartLimitIntervalSec`/`StartLimitBurst` moved from `[Service]` to `[Unit]` (systemd 240+ silently ignored them in `[Service]`). **PR [#684](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/684) (sq `18637c45`) V131 PR-A — CB-1..CB-4**: CB-1 `cmd_blacklist.sh` arithmetic crash, CB-2 `nftban_report_fhs.sh` undeclared associative array (`/etc/nftban` path-as-arithmetic), CB-3 `cmd_selftest.sh` printf/count (two occurrences), CB-4 `tests/selftest.sh` trace-path permission leak with per-user fallback. 15 deterministic tests. **PR [#685](https://github.com/itcmsgr/nftban/pull/685) (sq `488058ad`) V131 PR-A.2 — double-zero pattern sweep**: eliminated the `grep -c ... || echo "0"` / `|| echo 0` count-fallback bug class (`grep -c` prints `0` AND exits 1 on no-match, so `|| echo` appended a second `0`, producing `0\n0` that crashed `$((...))` arithmetic and `printf %d`). Both quoted and unquoted variants, plus quoted sites whose grep pattern itself contains a pipe, plus one arithmetic-inline hoist, swept across ~22 files with the safe idiom `|| true` + `${var:-0}`; a regression guard (`v131_pr_a_2_double_zero_sweep_test.sh`, 6/6) catches all four variants and forbids `grep -c` command-substitution directly inside `$((...))`. A pre-merge `sed` corruption was caught by `bash -n` and discarded before the squash; the merged result is clean (re-audited: zero remaining sites both variants, no corruption markers). **Validation:** per-PR CI green on all four (`#682` 55/3-skip, `#683` 51/4-skip, `#684` 50/2-skip, `#685` 51/2-skip); post-merge `main` CI green at `488058ad`; local suites green (sweep guard 6/6, V131 PR-A 15/15, V130 28/28, V129 17/17, V128 PR-A 23/23, V127 UX-2 40/40). **Package-install validation PENDING** — because `#682` changes the install payload + polkit surface, `EXECUTE_V1_131_0_PACKAGE_INSTALL_VALIDATION` (lab2 DEB → one RPM lab) is REQUIRED before this release is declared fully shipped; this is NOT a no-op packaging release and the v1.128/v1.129 "no systemd/polkit-rule change" guarantee does NOT apply to v1.131.0. **Schema 1.83.0 remains frozen** — no new validator field, no new metric, no new Prometheus label, no Status JSON wire-format key, no new `install_state` JSON field, no new nftables set/chain/table, no new config key. (Note: v1.131.0 DOES add a systemd unit + polkit rule entry — the schema-freeze covers wire-format/metrics/config keys, not the install payload.) **No host contact** during this release-prep construction. Only allowed files touched in this release-prep PR: `VERSION`, `STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `cli/lib/nftban/core/nftban_fhs_spec.sh` — the last is header-version regen only; FHS path-table body byte-unchanged (`build/fhs-spec.yaml` byte-equal). v1.131.x hotfix slot **not authorized** (latent reservation only).

`--yes` override path also verified), and **D8** (`cmd_firewall.sh::_firewall_validate_strict` accepts new `structural_ok` 2nd-arg and emits `STRICT PREFLIGHT: NOT VERIFIED` instead of `PASSED` when the structural validator failed first — UX contradiction resolved). New `cli/lib/nftban/tests/v129_pr_c_runtime_defect_fixes_test.sh` (17/17 PASS) + V127 UX-2 assertion 1.7 corrected (previously encoded the D7 bug as a feature). 55 CI checks PASS / 1 skipped / 0 fail at merge. **PR-D** (workspace-only) reverified the changed-case + smoke subset on all 3 hosts via lib-dir overlay (`NFTBAN_LIB_DIR=/tmp/v129-prd-lib`; production `/usr/lib/nftban` untouched; cleaned post-run): **D1, D4, D7, D8 RUNTIME-VERIFIED ✓ cross-family**; D5 **DOCUMENTED ✓**; D7 P0 **CLOSED cross-family**; D6.B **shell-VERIFIED ✓ + Go-PENDING-PACKAGE-BUILD** (the `validator.go` fix on `main @ 21ba927d` will activate end-to-end at runtime when this release's `nftban-validate` Go binary is rebuilt by the v1.129.0 package-build cycle); D6.A **NOT addressed — deferred to v1.130+** (polkit architecture expansion is a design decision: extend polkit to cover direct nft access via pkexec, OR document the gap honestly). **Honest scope of v1.129:** this release validates the representative §9 battery only — **NOT** all 77 cmd_*.sh files, **NOT** full subcommand/flag-level help/code correlation, **NOT** wiki/docs/README runtime alignment. Long-tail 77-command battery, subcommand/flag doctest, wiki/docs/README runtime audit, D7 live confirmation, D7 log/state-write footprint probe, operator-correction regression seeds C-2 (live well-known ban) and C-9 (mutating preflight) were **NOT in the authorized v1.129 scope** and remain available as separately-gated v1.130+ lanes if operator wants them. **Binary impact:** `cmd/nftban-core` differs from v1.128.0 in one localized addition at `internal/validator/validator.go::ValidateKernel` (permission-denied branch that scrubs upstream wording + substitutes CAP_NET_ADMIN remediation; function signature unchanged; capability check logic unchanged; non-permission code paths unchanged). `cmd/nftband` byte-identical to v1.128.0. **Prior release lane:** v1.128.0 (2026-05-24) — V128 **CLI text authority alignment release** on top of v1.127.0, bundling four narrow lanes filed in `AUDIT_190_LIFECYCLE/V128_CLI_TEXT_AUTHORITY_ALIGNMENT_SCOPE.md` and merged in strict order PR-A.1 → PR-A.2 → PR-B → PR-C → PR-D. Each lane was implemented, deterministic-tested, CI-clean-on-PR, and merged as a separately-shippable narrow PR before this release-prep PR was opened. **The canonical NFTBan privilege architecture is now enforced in code:** "Members of the nftban group can run supported NFTBan privileged operations through PolicyKit/polkit authorization rules." (Evidence basis: `packaging/polkit-1/rules.d/10-nftban-systemd.rules` uses `isInGroup("nftban")` to gate a whitelist of nftban-owned systemd unit management operations; `install/systemd/sysusers.d/nftban.conf` creates the `nftban` group alongside `nftban-auditor` and `nftban-panel`. The nftban-group + polkit IS the authorization path; root remains a technical fallback for bootstrap/installer scripts only.) **PR #675 (sq `b3b28121`) PR-A.1 — polkit-aware authority wording sweep**: 23 CLI shell files + 1 Go file (`cmd/nftban-core/privilege.go` error string only; capability check logic UNCHANGED, function signatures UNCHANGED) rewritten to the canonical statement. The three V127 UX-6 PR-F REQUIRES blocks that shipped in v1.127.0 with the wrong "Otherwise run as root or use the site-approved privilege method" wording were corrected in this PR. 20 deterministic test assertions added (A1-A7 forbidden patterns: zero "Run with sudo" / "Permission denied (not root" / "requires root" / "must run as root" / "site-approved privilege method" / "(sudo)" parenthetical; B1-B3 Go privilege.go uses polkit-aware framing + preserves CAP_NET_ADMIN technical guidance; C1-C5 destructive helps use canonical "nftban group" + "PolicyKit/polkit"; D1-D5 scope-creep guards). **PR #676 (sq `53ec9b61`) PR-A.2 — sudo nftban example sweep**: 68 main-CLI `sudo nftban X` example lines across 23 CLI shell files rewritten to `nftban X` (drop sudo prefix; privilege requirement documented once in REQUIRES block, not repeated in every example) + 14 residual operator-facing `root privileges` / `NEEDS ROOT` / `needs root` strings across 7 additional files rewritten to canonical. `sudo nftban-installer` and `sudo nftban-core` references preserved per installer/bootstrap allowlist. Test hardened from 20/20 to 23/23 (A5 example-line zero-tolerance + A8/A9 residual-wording guards added). **PR #677 (sq `44420367`) PR-B — top-level help/code correlation audit**: new deterministic harness asserting (C1) canonical command list extraction, (C2) cmd_*.sh file inventory, (C3) dispatcher's nftban-core case list, (C4) every cmd_<X>.sh maps to a canonical command, (C5) every canonical command is reachable via file OR dispatcher OR inline-handled allowlist. Audit caught real drift: 11 cmd_*.sh files (`benchmark`, `cleanup`, `egress`, `flush`, `preflight`, `pro`, `protect`, `scale`, `snapshot`, `tunnel`, `unprotect`) were missing from the canonical command list (typo handler was broken for those names since V127 UX-5); added to canonical heredoc, list grew 69 → 80 entries. 8 inline-dispatched commands (`install`, `uninstall`, `rebuild`, `enable`, `disable`, `restart`, `cloudflare`, `help`) added to explicit allowlist. **IMPORTANT SCOPE NOTE:** PR-B covers TOP-LEVEL command correlation ONLY. The deeper subcommand-level + flag-level + alias + SEE-ALSO correlation (the "help says X subcmd / code accepts Y subcmd" drift class) was intentionally deferred to v1.129 `DEEP_CLI_HELP_CODE_DOCTEST_CORRELATION` because naive global extraction produced noisy false positives across helper sub-modules, wildcard cases, and narrative help text during prototyping. v1.128 does NOT claim full help/code correlation coverage. **PR-C (wiki commit `312fdb3` on `nftban.wiki:master`) — wiki CLI text alignment**: 22 `sudo nftban X` example lines across 6 wiki .md files (`DNS-Tunnel-Suspicion.md`, `FHS-Compliance.md`, `GeoIP-Database-Guide.md`, `Security-Architecture.md`, `Suricata-IDS-Integration.md`, plus `Installation-Guide.md` preserving the allowlisted `sudo nftban-installer --repair` recovery command) rewritten to drop sudo prefix; 6 `sudo nftban-core geoip update` references rewritten to the canonical polkit-authorized CLI path `nftban geoip update`; 3 residual `requires root` / `root privileges` narrative strings in `FHS-Compliance.md` rewritten to canonical "elevated privileges + nftban group + PolicyKit/polkit" wording. In-repo `docs/**/*.md` (16 files) and `README.md` were baseline-clean (0 `sudo nftban` occurrences) so no Stage 2 main-repo PR was needed for PR-C. **PR #678 (sq `855c0f49`) PR-D — doc clarity lockable lint**: recon at branch-open found ZERO actionable clarity issues in README + docs/ (the V128 arc PR-A.1 + PR-A.2 + PR-B + PR-C work already brought operator-facing markdown to the canonical state);
